\displaystyle\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}+e^{-\frac{\rho}{2\varepsilon^{2}}}\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{b}}{x_{3}}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{-1}\left(\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(U_{3})^{2}\right\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(PU_{3})\right\|_{L^{2}}\right)\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}},

where
\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{b}
satisfies the bound in Lemma
3.8
.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi=\mathcal{I}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{2},

(3.45)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\left(|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}|^{2}+2\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\cdot({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right)dxd\xi.

Bounding
\mathcal{I}_{1}
:
We have

\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}=\frac{1}{2}\partial_{i}U_{j}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{j}+\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi.

(3.46)

This implies

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)||\varphi||({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi+o(1)\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}.

Now we bound the term

\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi.

We have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi

\displaystyle=\int_{|\varphi|\leq\varepsilon^{-1}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi+\int_{|\varphi|\geq\varepsilon^{-1}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}+\int_{|\varphi|\geq\varepsilon^{-1}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi.

It suffices to bound the second term in the above.
We have

\displaystyle\int_{|\varphi|\geq\varepsilon^{-1}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}\int_{|\varphi|\geq\varepsilon^{-1}}|\nabla_{x}U||\varphi|^{2}e^{-\rho|\xi|^{2}}|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|dxd\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{\rho}{2\varepsilon^{2}}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}\left\|e^{-\frac{\rho}{10}|\xi|^{2}}\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{\rho}{2\varepsilon^{2}}}\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}.

Hence we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}+e^{-\frac{\rho}{2\varepsilon^{2}}}\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}.

Bounding
\mathcal{I}_{2}
:
By a direct calculation, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}=\partial_{i}U_{j}b_{i,\alpha}b_{j,\alpha}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon a_{\alpha}b_{\alpha}\cdot(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)+\varepsilon c_{\alpha}b_{\alpha}\cdot(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)+2a_{\alpha}\partial_{i}U_{j}\langle({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha},\hat{A}_{ij}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+b_{m,\alpha}\left\{\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U_{k}+U_{i}\partial_{i}U_{k})\langle\hat{A}_{mk},({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\partial_{i}U_{k}\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k}\varphi_{m}\sqrt{\mu},({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}c_{\alpha}\left\{\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U_{k}+U\cdot\nabla U_{k})\langle\varphi_{k}(|\varphi|^{2}-3)\sqrt{\mu},({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}c_{\alpha}\left\{\partial_{i}U_{k}\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k}(|\varphi|^{2}-3)\sqrt{\mu},({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}},|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}|^{2}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{2}\left|\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}U_{j}b_{\alpha,3}b_{\alpha,j}\right|+\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}+o(1)\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}.

It suffices to bound the term
\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}U_{j}b_{\alpha,3}b_{\alpha,j}
for
j\in\{1,2\}
. Using Lemma
3.8
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}U_{j}b_{\alpha,3}b_{\alpha,j}=\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}U_{j}(\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{b}-\varepsilon U_{3}a_{\alpha}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2\delta}\partial^{\alpha}(U_{3}^{2})-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}(PU_{3}))b_{\alpha,j}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|x_{3}\partial_{3}U_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{b}}{x_{3}}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{-1}\left(\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(U_{3})^{2}\right\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(PU_{3})\right\|_{L^{2}}\right)\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

As a corollary, we have

Corollary 3.10
.

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+e^{-\frac{\rho}{4{\varepsilon}^{2}}}\mathcal{H}_{f}(t)\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+o(1)\mathcal{Y}_{f}(t)^{2}.

Proof.

From the estimate in the above lemma
3.9
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha}A_{\alpha}^{2}\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+e^{-\frac{\rho}{4{\varepsilon}^{2}}}\mathcal{H}_{f}(t)\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{b}}{x_{3}}\right\|_{L^{2}}.

Hence it suffices to estimate

\mathcal{I}=\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\frac{\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{b}}{x_{3}}\right\|_{L^{2}}.

Now using Lemma
3.8
, we get

\mathcal{I}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{2}+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{\partial\in\{\partial_{x_{1}},\partial_{x_{2}}\}}\left\|\partial f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\sum_{\partial\in\{\varepsilon\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{1}},\partial_{x_{2}}\}}\left\|\partial f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\left\|e^{o(1)|\xi|^{2}}(1,\varepsilon\partial_{t},\nabla_{x})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Bounding
\mathcal{I}_{1}
:
Using the fact that

\frac{\tau}{\sqrt{(|\alpha|+1)(|\alpha|+2)}}\max\left\{\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}}{\mathcal{A}_{(\alpha_{0}+1,\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})}},\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}}{\mathcal{A}_{(\alpha_{0},\alpha_{1}+1,\alpha_{2})}},\frac{\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}}{\mathcal{A}_{(\alpha_{0},\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}+1)}}\right\}\leq 1,

we obtain

\mathcal{I}_{1}\lesssim\mathcal{Y}_{f}(t)^{2}.

Bounding
\mathcal{I}_{2}
: First, we note that for any non-negative sequences
\{x_{\alpha}\},\{y_{\alpha}\},\{z_{\alpha}\}
, we have

\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}x_{\alpha}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}y_{\beta}z_{\gamma}\lesssim\left(\sum_{\alpha}\frac{|\alpha|+1}{\tau}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}x_{\alpha}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{\alpha}\frac{|\alpha|+1}{\tau}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}y_{\alpha}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{|\gamma|\geq 1}\frac{\tau^{|\gamma|}}{\gamma!}z_{\gamma}

by the discrete Young inequality and the fact that

\frac{\alpha!\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}}{\gamma!\mathcal{A}_{\gamma}}\lesssim\tau^{-1}\beta!\min\left\{\mathcal{A}_{(\beta_{0}+1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2})},\mathcal{A}_{(\beta_{0},\beta_{1}+1,\beta_{2})},\mathcal{A}_{(\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}+1)}\right\}.

This, combining with the bound of
\mu
in Lemma
(
2.3
)
, gives us the bound
\mathcal{I}_{2}\lesssim o(1)\mathcal{Y}_{f}(t)^{2}
. The proof is complete.
∎

Proposition 3.11
.

[Commutator terms] There holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}(t)\langle[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f,f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\left\|\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}(t)\langle[L,\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}]f,\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)

\displaystyle+o(1)\mathcal{D}_{\partial_{t}f}(t)^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{\partial_{t}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{\partial_{t}f}(t),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\left\|\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}[L,\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{\partial_{t}f}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{\partial_{t}f}(t).

Proof.

Recalling
(
3.4
)
and the fact that
\partial^{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})=({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\alpha}+[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\alpha}]
, we decompose

[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f=\sum_{i=1}^{8}T_{\alpha,i},

where

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

(3.47)

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\varrho}]f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\varrho}(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,4}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\varrho}]f(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,5}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,6}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\varrho}]f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,7}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\gamma}(\xi)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,8}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\gamma}]f(\xi)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}.

We have

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\langle[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f,f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{8}\langle\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}T_{\alpha,i},\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{8}\left\{\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}T_{\alpha,i}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}T_{\alpha,i}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\}.

This implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\langle[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f,f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq 8}\left\{\sum_{\alpha}\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}T_{\alpha,i}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\varepsilon^{-1}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\varepsilon^{-2}\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\right).

We now bound each of the term
T_{\alpha,i}
. Using Proposition
5.9
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{A}_{\gamma}\left\|e^{p_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\mathcal{A}_{\varrho}f_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{A}_{\beta}\left\|e^{p_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{\gamma}\left\|e^{p_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdot\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\mathcal{A}_{\varrho}f_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{A}_{\gamma}\left\|e^{p_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\mathcal{A}_{\varrho}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\varrho}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{A}_{\beta}\left\|e^{p_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdot\mathcal{A}_{\gamma}\left\|e^{p_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\mathcal{A}_{\varrho}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\varrho}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

Now using the discrete Young inequality, Lemma
2.3
and Proposition
5.18
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}+\left\|\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{E}_{f}(t).

Similarly, using Proposition
5.10
, Lemma
2.3
, and Proposition
5.18
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}+\left\|\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{E}_{f}(t).

As for
T_{\alpha,5}
and
T_{\alpha,6}
, we use Proposition
5.11
to get

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\alpha,5}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|T_{\alpha,6}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|+|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\left\{\mathcal{A}_{\varrho}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\mathcal{A}_{\varrho}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\varrho}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\beta}\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}\nu(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\mathcal{A}_{\gamma}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Now using the discrete Young inequality, Lemma
2.3
, Proposition
5.18
and Proposition
5.11
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{5\leq m\leq 8}\left\|\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{\alpha,m}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{E}_{f}(t).

Hence we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\langle[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f,f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\right)\left(\varepsilon^{-1}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\varepsilon^{-2}\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}+\varepsilon\kappa\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}.

The commutative estimate for time derivative is similar, where there are two cases:
\partial_{t}
hits
\sqrt{\mu}
, which gives a scale of
\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}
, and when
\partial_{t}
hits
f
, which gives us the same bound for
f
without any derivatives.
This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 3.12
.

There holds, for
\varepsilon\ll\kappa

\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\varepsilon^{-1}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\right\}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,\ell}\partial_{i}\partial^{\alpha}U_{\ell}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{\ell}+J_{\alpha}

where

\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}|J_{\alpha}|\lesssim o(1)\langle\varphi\rangle.

Proof.

Recalling
(
3.46
)
, we get

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\varepsilon^{-1}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\partial_{i}\partial^{\alpha}U_{\ell}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\\
|\varrho|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{i}U_{\ell}\partial^{\gamma}\varphi_{i}\partial^{\varrho}\varphi_{\ell}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\\
\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{i}U_{\ell}\partial^{\gamma}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{\ell}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\\
\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{i}U_{\ell}\partial^{\gamma}\varphi_{\ell}\varphi_{i}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\beta}(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\partial^{\gamma}\varphi+\varepsilon\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\partial_{i}\partial^{\alpha}U_{\ell}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{\ell}+J_{\alpha}.

where

\displaystyle J_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\\
|\varrho|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{i}U_{\ell}|\cdot\varepsilon|\partial^{\gamma}U_{i}|\cdot\varepsilon|\partial^{\varrho}U_{\ell}|+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\\
\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{i}U_{\ell}|\cdot\varepsilon|\partial^{\gamma}U_{i}||\varphi|

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\\
\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{i}U_{\ell}|\cdot\varepsilon|\partial^{\gamma}U_{\ell}||\varphi|+\varepsilon\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}|\partial^{\beta}(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)|\cdot\varepsilon|\partial^{\gamma}U|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon|\partial^{\alpha}(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)||\varphi|.

This implies

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}J_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\left\|U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\varepsilon\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}\left\|U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\left(\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}\left\|U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\left\|\partial_{t}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right)|\varphi|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2}\left\|U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}\left\|\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\langle\varphi\rangle.

This completes the proof.∎

Proposition 3.13
.

There holds

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\left\langle\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f_{\alpha}-\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f\right\},f_{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+o(1)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}+\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|x_{3}^{-1}T_{\beta}^{b}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|x_{3}\partial_{3}\partial^{\gamma}U_{\parallel}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|x_{3}^{-1}T_{\alpha}^{b}\right\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|x_{3}\partial_{3}\partial^{\gamma}U_{\parallel}\right\|_{L^{\infty}},

where
T_{\alpha}^{b}
satisfies the bound in Lemma
3.8
.

Proof.

In the proof of this proposition, let us denote

S_{\alpha}=\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\langle\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f_{\alpha}-\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f\right\},f_{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

We have

\displaystyle\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f_{\alpha}-\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f\right\}

(3.48)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\beta<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\gamma}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\right\}\partial^{\beta}f.

Combining the above with Lemma
3.12
, we have

S_{\alpha}=S_{\alpha,1}+S_{\alpha,2}

where

\displaystyle S_{\alpha,1}

\displaystyle=-\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\beta<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\langle J_{\gamma},f_{\beta}f_{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

(3.49)

\displaystyle S_{\alpha,2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\beta<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\sum_{i,k}\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{\gamma}U_{k}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},f_{\beta}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

First we have

\displaystyle\langle J_{\gamma},f_{\beta}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}J_{\gamma}f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu^{-1}J_{\gamma}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

Hence

\left\|S_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{1}}\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\sqrt{\nu}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{\infty}}\left\|\nu^{-1}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}J_{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\sqrt{\nu}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+o(1)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}.

Next we bound
S_{\alpha,2}
. We have

\displaystyle\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},f_{\beta}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle=\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},\mathbf{P}f_{\beta}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},\mathbf{P}f_{\beta}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\beta}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\beta}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

This gives us the decomposition

S_{\alpha,2}=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=1}^{4}T_{\alpha,k}

where

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{\gamma}U_{k},\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\beta}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{\gamma}U_{k},\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k}\mathbf{P}f_{\beta}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,3}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{\gamma}U_{k},\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\beta}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

\displaystyle T_{\alpha,4}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\partial_{i}\partial^{\gamma}U_{k}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\beta}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

First we bound
\left\|T_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{1}}
. To this end, we find

\displaystyle\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\beta}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}=a_{\alpha}a_{\beta}\delta_{ik}+3(a_{\alpha}c_{\beta}+a_{\beta}c_{\alpha})\delta_{ik}

\displaystyle+b_{\alpha,m}b_{\beta,n}\left(\delta_{ik}\delta_{mn}+\delta_{im}\delta_{kn}+\delta_{in}\delta_{km}\right)+c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}\delta_{ik}\left\langle|\varphi|^{2}\mu,\left(\frac{|\varphi|^{2}-3}{2}\right)^{2}\right\rangle.

This implies

\displaystyle\sum_{i,k}\partial_{i}\partial^{\gamma}U_{k}\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha},\mathbf{P}f_{\beta}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle=a_{\alpha}a_{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}(\nabla_{x}\cdot U)+O(1)(a_{\alpha}c_{\beta}+a_{\beta}c_{\alpha})\partial^{\gamma}(\nabla_{x}\cdot U)

\displaystyle+\partial^{\gamma}(\nabla_{x}\cdot U)(b_{\alpha}\cdot b_{\beta})+\partial^{\gamma}\left(\partial_{k}U_{i}+\partial_{k}U_{i}\right)b_{\alpha,i}b_{\beta,k}

\displaystyle+O(1)c_{\alpha}c_{\beta}\partial^{\gamma}(\nabla_{x}\cdot U).

Hence

\displaystyle\sum_{i,k}\partial_{i}\partial^{\gamma}U_{k}\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{k},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha},\mathbf{P}f_{\beta}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}({\rm div}U)\right\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\left|\partial^{\gamma}\left(\partial_{k}U_{i}+\partial_{i}U_{k}\right)b_{\alpha,i}b_{\beta,k}\right|.

Hence we get

\left\|T_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{1}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\max_{1\leq i\leq 2}\left|\int_{\Omega}\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{3}U_{i}(b_{\alpha,i}b_{\beta,3}+b_{\alpha,3}b_{\beta,i})dx\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

Now for
T_{\alpha,2},T_{\alpha,3}
and
T_{\alpha,4}
, it is straight forward that

\max_{1\leq i\leq 2}\left\|T_{\alpha,i}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim o(1)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)\mathcal{E}_{f}(t),\qquad\left\|T_{\alpha,4}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim o(1)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}.

Here, the key is that we have used the dissipation gain of scale
\varepsilon\sqrt{\kappa}
to absorb the growth in
\kappa
for the analytic norm of
\partial_{x_{3}}U
, which can only grow at most
\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}
for a general Prandtl flow.
Now we will bound the most difficult term, which is

\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\left|\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}\partial^{\gamma}U_{1}(b_{\alpha,1}b_{\beta,3}+b_{\alpha,3}b_{\beta,1})dx\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

Remark 3.14
.

We note that since
|\gamma|\geq 1
and
U
is a shear flow, we have
\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}U_{1}\sim\sqrt{\kappa}
, which is a good term. For a general flow that is not a shear flow, this is not the case. However, using the same technique as in the proof of Proposition
3.9
, we will show that we do not shear condition for this term, by making use of the analyticity and conservation laws. This observation will be important for our future works.

Now to treat the above term, we use Lemma
3.8
and decompose

\displaystyle\partial_{3}\partial^{\gamma}U_{1}\left(b_{\alpha,1}b_{\beta,3}+b_{\alpha,3}b_{\beta,1}\right)

\displaystyle=(\partial_{3}\partial^{\gamma}U_{1})b_{\alpha,1}\left\{\mathcal{T}^{b}_{\beta}-\varepsilon U_{3}a_{\beta}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2\delta}\partial^{\beta}\{U_{3}^{2}\}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\partial^{\beta}(PU_{3})\right\}

\displaystyle+(\partial_{3}\partial^{\gamma}U_{1})b_{\beta,1}\left\{\mathcal{T}^{b}_{\alpha}-\varepsilon U_{3}a_{\alpha}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2\delta}\partial^{\alpha}\{U_{3}^{2}\}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}(PU_{3})\right\}.

Hence

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}\partial^{\gamma}U_{1}\left(b_{\alpha,1}b_{\beta,3}+b_{\alpha,3}b_{\beta,1}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{3}U_{1}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left(\varepsilon\left\|f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\delta^{-1}\left\|\partial^{\beta}(U_{3}^{2})\right\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\delta^{-1}\left\|\partial^{\beta}(PU_{2})\right\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{3}U_{1}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left(\varepsilon\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\delta^{-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(U_{3}^{2})\right\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\delta^{-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(PU_{2})\right\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|x_{3}\partial_{3}\partial^{\gamma}U_{1}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\{\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|x_{3}^{-1}T_{\beta}^{b}\right\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|x_{3}^{-1}T_{\alpha}^{b}\right\|_{L^{2}}\right\}.

The proof is complete.∎

Theorem 3.15
.

Recall
(
3.7
)
. Let
f
be the solution to the equation
(
3.43
)
with the boundary condition
(
3.24
)
and
(
3.25
)
. There exists a constant
T>0
independent of
\kappa
and
\varepsilon
such that, for all
0\leq t\leq T
, there holds

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+O(1)\mathcal{D}(t)^{2}+O(1)\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-P_{\gamma_{+}})\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\delta^{-2}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}r\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left|\langle g_{\alpha},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|+\left\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta}\mathcal{E}_{\nabla_{x}\cdot U}(t)+1\right\}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t).

Here the radius of analyticity is given by

\tau(t)=\tau_{0}-2\tau_{0}Tt.

Proof.

Recalling
(
3.7
)
, we have

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}=\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\langle f_{\alpha},\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}}+\tau^{\prime}(t)\mathcal{Y}_{f}(t)^{2}.

(3.50)

Here we used the fact that

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}=\tau^{\prime}(t)\frac{|\alpha|+1}{\tau}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}.

Using the equation
(
3.32
)
and
(
3.50
)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)-\tau^{\prime}(t)\mathcal{Y}_{f}(t)^{2}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\alpha},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}-\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\langle Lf_{\alpha},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}dxd\xi

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\langle[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f,f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\langle g_{\alpha},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\left\langle\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f_{\alpha}-\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f\right\},f_{\alpha}\right\rangle.

It suffices to bound the boundary term, all the other terms are treated in Propositions
3.13
,
3.11
, Corollary
3.10
.
We have

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\alpha},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\varepsilon}\int_{\partial\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}d\gamma=\frac{1}{2{\varepsilon}}\int_{\gamma_{+}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}d\gamma-\frac{1}{2{\varepsilon}}\int_{\gamma_{-}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}d\gamma

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2{\varepsilon}}\int_{\gamma_{+}}|f_{\alpha}|^{2}d\gamma-\frac{1}{2{\varepsilon}}\int_{\gamma_{-}}\left|\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}-\frac{{\varepsilon}}{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}r\right|^{2}d\gamma

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2{\varepsilon}}\int_{\gamma_{+}}\left(|f_{\alpha}|^{2}-|\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}|^{2}\right)d\gamma-\frac{\varepsilon}{2\delta^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}r\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\delta}\int_{\gamma_{-}}\left(\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}\right)\partial^{\alpha}rd\gamma

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2{\varepsilon}}\int_{\gamma_{+}}\left|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right|^{2}d\gamma-\frac{\varepsilon}{2\delta^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}r\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\delta}\int_{\gamma_{-}}\left(\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}\right)\partial^{\alpha}rd\gamma.

Now we will show that the third term in the above is zero:

\int_{\gamma_{-}}\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}rd\gamma=0.

To this end, we note that

\int_{\gamma_{-}}\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}rd\gamma=\int_{\xi_{3}>0}z_{f_{\alpha}}(x)c_{\mu}\sqrt{\mu_{0}(\xi)}\cdot(\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}(t,x,\xi)-z_{\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}}(x)c_{\mu}\sqrt{\mu_{0}(\xi)})|\xi_{3}|dS(x)d\xi,

where

z_{f_{\alpha}}(x)=\int_{\xi_{3}<0}f_{\alpha}(t,x,\xi)\sqrt{\mu_{0}(\xi)}|\xi_{3}|d\xi,\quad z_{\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}}(x)=\int_{\xi_{3}<0}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}(t,x,\xi)\sqrt{\mu_{0}(\xi)}|\xi_{3}|d\xi.

Hence

\displaystyle\int_{\gamma_{-}}\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}\cdot\partial^{\alpha}rd\gamma

(3.51)

\displaystyle=\int_{\partial\Omega}c_{\mu}z_{f_{\alpha}}(x)\left\{\int_{\xi_{3}>0}\sqrt{\mu_{0}(\xi)}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}(t,x,\xi)|\xi_{3}|d\xi-z_{\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}}(x)c_{\mu}\int_{\xi_{3}>0}\mu_{0}(\xi)|\xi_{3}|d\xi\right\}dS(x).

Since
\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}(t,x,\xi)|_{x_{3}=0}=\partial^{\alpha}P|_{x_{3}=0}\sqrt{\mu}_{0}(\xi)-\kappa\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{i}u_{j}A_{ij}(\xi).

We note that
A_{i3}(\xi)
is odd in
\xi_{1},\xi_{2}
for
i\neq 3
and
A_{33}(\xi)
is even in
\xi_{3}
. Therefore

z_{\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}}(x)=\int_{\xi_{3}<0}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}(t,x,\xi)\sqrt{\mu_{0}(\xi)}|\xi_{3}|d\xi=\int_{\xi_{3}>0}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}(t,x,\xi)\sqrt{\mu_{0}(\xi)}|\xi_{3}|d\xi.

Hence from
(
3.51
)
and above identity, we get

\int_{\gamma_{-}}\left(\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}\right)\partial^{\alpha}rd\gamma=0.

In the end, we have

\displaystyle-\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\alpha},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2{\varepsilon}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\int_{\gamma_{+}}\left|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right|^{2}d\gamma

(3.52)

\displaystyle-\frac{{\varepsilon}}{2\delta^{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\|\partial^{\alpha}r\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}.

The proof is complete.
∎

3.7
The
L^{2}
nonlinear energy estimate

In this section, we estimate the term
\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left|\langle g_{\alpha},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|
appearing in Theorem
3.15
. We estimate the term
-\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta\sqrt{\mu}}\textbf{R}_{a}
in Proposition
3.17
, the term
\frac{2}{\kappa}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},f)
in Proposition
3.18

nonlinear term in Proposition
3.16
.

Proposition 3.16
.

[Estimate of the nonlinear term] Recalling the norms in
(
3.7
)
,
there holds

\displaystyle\frac{\delta}{\kappa\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}(t)^{2}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,f),\partial^{\alpha}f\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}ds

(3.53)

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\right)\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\delta\varepsilon\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(s).

Proof.

Recalling the definition of
\Gamma(f,f)
, we have

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,f)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\Gamma(f_{\beta},f_{\gamma})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left(f_{\gamma}(\xi^{\prime})f_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+f_{\gamma}(\xi^{\prime})f_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left(f_{\gamma}(\xi)f_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star})+f_{\gamma}(\xi)f_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}.

This implies

\displaystyle\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,f),f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\gamma}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\gamma}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{o(1)|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\gamma}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{o(1)|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\gamma}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}.

Using the discrete Young inequality, the estimate of the local Maxwellian in Lemma
2.3
and the fact that

\begin{cases}\frac{\alpha!\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}}{\beta!\mathcal{A}_{\beta}\gamma!\mathcal{A}_{\gamma}}&\lesssim\langle\gamma\rangle^{-9}1_{\{|\beta|\geq|\gamma|\}}+\langle\beta\rangle^{-9}1_{\{|\gamma|\geq|\beta|\}},\\
\frac{\alpha!\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}}{\beta!\mathcal{A}_{\beta}\gamma!\mathcal{A}_{\gamma}\varrho!\mathcal{A}_{\varrho}}&\lesssim\langle\gamma\rangle^{-9}1_{\{|\varrho|\geq|\gamma|\}}+\langle\varrho\rangle^{-9}1_{\{|\gamma|\geq|\varrho|\}},\end{cases}

(3.54)

we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}(t)\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,f),f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\varepsilon\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Next we bound the error term in both the
L^{2}
analytic and
L^{\infty}
analytic estimates. To simplify the statement of the theorem, we define following functions:

\displaystyle d_{D}(t)=\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{\delta}\left(\||P\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\kappa\||\nabla_{x}U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right)^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\delta}\||\nabla_{x}^{2}U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty},

\displaystyle d_{E}(t)=\frac{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{3}}{\delta}\left\|\textbf{NS}(U,P)\right\|_{\tau_{0},2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta}\left\|{\rm div}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},2}+\frac{\kappa\varepsilon}{\delta}\||\nabla_{x}U\||_{\tau_{0},2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\left\{\||\partial_{t}P+U\cdot\nabla_{x}P\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\varepsilon\||P(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\||P\partial_{i}U_{j}\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon\kappa}{\delta}\left(\||(\partial_{t}+U\cdot\nabla_{x})(\partial_{i}U_{j})\||_{\tau_{0},2}+\varepsilon\||(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)(\partial_{i}U_{j})\||_{\tau_{0},2}+\||\nabla_{x}U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\||\nabla_{x}U\||_{\tau_{0},2}\right).

Proposition 3.17

(Estimate of the forcing term)
.

Recalling the formula of
\textbf{R}_{a}
in
(
3.27
)
, as long as
|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\ll 1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}(t)\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta\sqrt{\mu}}\textbf{R}_{a}\right),f_{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}ds\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2},

under the assumption

d_{E}(t)+d_{D}(t)\lesssim 1.

Proof.

Now we bound
L^{2}
norm. From
(
3.27
)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta\sqrt{\mu}}\textbf{R}_{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\delta}\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\kappa\delta}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},{\bf{f_{2}}})+\frac{1}{3\varepsilon\delta}{\rm div}U|\varphi|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}-\frac{\kappa}{\delta}\partial_{i\ell}U_{j}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})(\varphi_{\ell}A_{ij})

(3.55)

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\sqrt{\mu}\left\{(\partial_{t}P+U\cdot\nabla_{x}P)+\varepsilon P(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi+\frac{1}{2}P\partial_{i}U_{\ell}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{\ell}\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{\varepsilon\kappa}{\delta}\left\{(\partial_{t}+U\cdot\nabla_{x})(\partial_{i}U_{j})+\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi\partial_{i}U_{j}+\partial_{i}U_{j}\partial_{k}U_{\ell}\varphi_{k}\varphi_{\ell}\right\}A_{ij}

\displaystyle-\frac{\kappa\varepsilon}{\delta}\partial_{i}U_{j}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}+(\varepsilon^{2}U+\varphi)\cdot\nabla_{x}\right)A_{ij}.

First we see that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\delta}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}(t)\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu}\right\},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\delta}\left\{\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}(t)\left\|\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t).

Using Lemma
2.3
for the analytic pointwise bound of
\mu
, we get

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\left\|\textbf{NS}(U,P)\right\|_{\tau_{0},2}e^{-\frac{1}{10}|\xi|^{2}}.

Combining the above two inequalities, we get

\frac{1}{\delta}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu}\right\},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\delta}\left\|\textbf{NS}(U,P)\right\|_{\tau_{0},2}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t).

Next we bound the second term coming from
(
3.55
)
. Using the trilinear estimate in Proposition
5.8
and then Lemma
2.3
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon}{\kappa\delta}\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},{\bf{f_{2}}}),f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\delta^{-1}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha}\frac{\tau_{0}^{2|\alpha|}}{\alpha!^{2}}\left\|\nu\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\delta^{-1}\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\||P\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\kappa\||\nabla_{x}U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right)^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\right).

Similarly, we get

\frac{1}{3\varepsilon\delta}\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left\{{\rm div}U|\varphi|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}\right\},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}ds\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta}\left\|{\rm div}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},2}\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}.

Next we bound

\frac{\kappa}{\delta}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\partial_{i\ell}U_{j}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})(\varphi_{\ell}A_{ij})\right\},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

Applying Proposition
5.14
to the above term and then use Corollary
5.24
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{\delta}\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\partial_{i\ell}U_{j}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})(\varphi_{\ell}A_{ij})\right\},f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\delta}\||\nabla_{x}^{2}U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\left(\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)\right).

Finally, the last three terms can be bounded by the same argument as in the above terms. We skip the details. The proof is complete.

∎

Proposition 3.18
.

There holds

\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left(\frac{2}{\kappa}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},f)\right),f_{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}ds\lesssim o(1)\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{\kappa}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)^{2}.

Proof.

Using Proposition
5.8
, discrete Young inequality and then Lemma
2.3
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left(\frac{2}{\kappa}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},f)\right),f_{\alpha}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\kappa}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\cdot\left\{\sqrt{\kappa}\varepsilon\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\kappa\varepsilon^{2}\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)^{2}+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\right\}.

It suffices to bound the terms involving
{\bf{f_{2}}}
in the above. We note that

\displaystyle\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\left(\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\beta|}}{\beta!}\left\|\partial^{\beta}P\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\gamma|}}{\gamma!}\left\|\nu\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}+\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\beta|}}{\beta!}\kappa\left\|\partial^{\beta}\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\gamma|}}{\gamma!}\left\|\nu\partial^{\gamma}A_{ij}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim 1

by Proposition
5.24
.
The proof is complete.
∎

Combining the above bounds on the forcing term and Theorem
3.15
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+O(1)\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}(s)^{2}ds+O(1)\varepsilon^{-1}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\alpha}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}r\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}^{2}+\left\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta}\left\||\nabla_{x}\cdot U\right\||_{\tau_{0},2}+\delta^{-1}\||\textbf{NS}(U)\||_{\tau_{0},2}\right\}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\right)\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t).

3.8
L^{\infty}
estimates

In this section, we derive the
L^{\infty}
for
f
solving
(
3.23
)
, or
F
solving
(
1.1
)
.

f=\frac{F-\mu-\varepsilon^{2}{\bf{f_{2}}}\sqrt{\mu}}{\varepsilon\delta\sqrt{\mu}}=\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}{\sqrt{\mu}}h.

We now focus on bounding the analytic
L^{\infty}
norm of
h
. We define

\mathcal{H}_{h}(t)=\left\{\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}(t)\left\|\partial^{\alpha}h\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}^{2}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(3.56)

We note that

\begin{cases}\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}{\sqrt{\mu}}&=T_{M}^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4T_{M}}+\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}-\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi+\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}=T_{M}^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}e^{\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}\\
\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}{\sqrt{\mu}_{0}}&=T_{M}^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}.\end{cases}

(3.57)

Here we note that
T_{M}^{-1}>1
.
Recalling the definition of the analytic
L^{\infty}
norm of
f
in
(
3.7
)
, we show the following lemma:

Lemma 3.19
.

For
\rho>0
and
T_{M}\in(0,1)
such that

\rho\ll|T_{M}^{-1}-1|,

there holds

\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{H}_{h}(t).

Proof.

We recall that
f=\frac{\sqrt{\mu}_{M}}{\sqrt{\mu}}h
. Using
(
3.57
)
, we have, for each
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
:

\displaystyle e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\frac{1}{\alpha!}\partial^{\alpha}f

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1-4\rho_{0})}\sum_{\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha}\frac{1}{\beta!}\left|\partial^{\beta}\left\{e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}\right\}\right|\cdot\frac{1}{\gamma!}\left|\partial^{\gamma}\left(e^{\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}\right)\right|\cdot\frac{1}{\varrho!}\left|\partial^{\varrho}h\right|.

(3.58)

Using Lemma
5.5
we get

\sup_{\beta\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\beta|}}{\beta!}\left|\partial^{\beta}\left\{e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}}\right\}\right|\lesssim e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}+4\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}|\xi_{\ell}|}\lesssim e^{\varepsilon|U|_{L^{\infty}}|\xi|}.

(3.59)

Combining
(
3.58
)
,
(
3.59
)
and the fact that
e^{\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}
is analytic in
x_{\parallel}
, we get the result, as long as

\rho+\varepsilon|U|_{L^{\infty}}\ll|T_{M}^{-1}-1|.

The proof is complete.
∎

We recall the equation for
h_{\alpha}=\partial^{\alpha}h
in
(
3.35
)
. We define the backward exit time and position as

t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,\xi)=\sup\left\{\tau\geq 0:x-\tau\frac{\xi}{\varepsilon}\in\bar{\Omega}\right\},\qquad x_{\mathbf{b}}(x,\xi)=x-\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,\xi)}{\varepsilon}\xi.

When
\Omega=\mathbb{T}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}_{+}
, one gets the explicit formula

t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,\xi)=\varepsilon\frac{x_{3}}{\xi_{3}},\qquad x_{\mathbf{b}}(x,\xi)=x-\frac{x_{3}}{\xi_{3}}\xi.

We also define the following notations

\begin{cases}-\int_{s}^{t}\nu_{M}&=-\int_{s}^{t}\nu_{M}(\tau,x-\frac{t-\tau}{{\varepsilon}}\xi,\xi)d\tau,\\
-\int_{s}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\nu_{M}&=-\int_{s}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\nu_{M}(\tau,x_{\mathbf{b}}-\frac{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}-\tau}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star})d\tau.\end{cases}

In the next proposition, we give a complete trajectory presentation for
h_{\alpha}
solving the equation
(
3.35
)
with the boundary condition
(
3.36
)
.
To this end, we define the following operators

\mathcal{I}(h_{\alpha,0})=\mathcal{I}_{1}(h_{\alpha,0})+\mathcal{I}_{2}(h_{\alpha,0}),

(3.60)

where

\mathcal{I}_{1}(h_{\alpha,0})=1_{\{t\leq t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\nu_{M}}h_{\alpha,0}\left(x-\frac{t}{{\varepsilon}}\xi,\xi\right),

(3.61)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}(h_{\alpha,0})

\displaystyle=1_{\{t\geq t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}\nu_{M}}w_{1}(\xi)

(3.62)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\nu_{M}}h_{\alpha,0}\left(x_{\mathbf{b}}-\frac{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)w_{2}(\xi_{\star})d\xi_{\star}.

The operator
\mathcal{M}(h_{\alpha})
is defined as

\mathcal{M}(h_{\alpha})=\mathcal{M}_{1}(h_{\alpha})+\mathcal{M}_{2}(h_{\alpha})

(3.63)

where

\mathcal{M}_{1}(h_{\alpha})=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\nu_{M}}K_{M}h_{\alpha}\left(s,x-\frac{t-s}{{\varepsilon}}\xi,\xi\right)ds,

(3.64)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{2}(h_{\alpha})

\displaystyle=1_{\{t\geq t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}\nu_{M}}w_{1}(\xi)

(3.65)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{0}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\nu_{M}}K_{M}h_{\alpha}\left(s,x_{\mathbf{b}}-\frac{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}-s}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)w_{2}(\xi_{\star})dsd\xi_{\star}.

The operator
\mathcal{F}
is defined as

\mathcal{F}(\widetilde{g}_{\alpha})=\mathcal{F}_{1}(\widetilde{g}_{\alpha})+\mathcal{F}_{2}(\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}),

(3.66)

where

\mathcal{F}_{1}(\widetilde{g}_{\alpha})=\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\nu_{M}}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}\left(s,x-\frac{t-s}{{\varepsilon}}\xi,\xi\right)ds,

(3.67)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{2}(\widetilde{g}_{\alpha})=1_{\{t>t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}\nu_{M}}w_{1}(\xi)

(3.68)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\xi_{\star}}\int_{0}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\nu_{M}}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}\left(s,x_{\mathbf{b}}-\frac{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}-s}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)w_{2}(\xi_{\star})dsd\xi_{\star}.

The boundary term is given by

\mathcal{B}(r_{\alpha})=1_{\{t\geq t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}w_{3}(\xi)^{-1}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}\nu_{M}}\partial^{\alpha}r(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},\xi).

(3.69)

Proposition 3.20
.

Let
h_{\alpha}
solves
(
3.35
)
with the boundary condition
(
3.36
)
and initial data
h_{\alpha,0}
. There holds

h_{\alpha}(t,x,\xi)=\mathcal{I}(h_{\alpha,0})+\mathcal{M}(h_{\alpha})+\mathcal{F}(\widetilde{g}_{\alpha})-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}T_{M}^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathcal{B}(r_{\alpha})

where
\mathcal{I},\mathcal{M},\mathcal{F}
are defined in
(
3.60
)
,
(
3.63
)
and
(
3.66
)
.

Proof.

Denoting
t_{\mathbf{b}}=t_{\mathbf{b}}(x,\xi),x_{\mathbf{b}}=x_{\mathbf{b}}(x,\xi)
. Using the Duhamel principle for the equation

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}h_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa}\nu_{M}h_{\alpha}=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}K_{M}h_{\alpha}+\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle h_{\alpha}|_{\xi_{3}>0}=c_{\mu}w_{1}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}h_{\alpha}(t,x,\xi_{\star})w_{2}(\xi_{\star})d\xi_{\star}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}w_{3}(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}r,

we get

\displaystyle h_{\alpha}(t,x,\xi)

\displaystyle=1_{\{t<t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\nu_{M}}h_{\alpha,0}\left(x-\frac{t}{{\varepsilon}}\xi,\xi\right)

(3.70)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\nu_{M}}K_{M}h_{\alpha}\left(s,x-\frac{t-s}{{\varepsilon}}\xi,\xi\right)ds

\displaystyle+\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\nu_{M}}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}\left(s,x-\frac{t-s}{{\varepsilon}}\xi,\xi\right)ds

\displaystyle+1_{\{t>t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}\nu_{M}}h_{\alpha}\left(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},\xi\right).

From the equation
(
3.70
)
, we obtain the term
\mathcal{I}_{1}
and
\mathcal{F}_{1}
directly. We now write the boundary term on the forth line in
(
3.70
)
, using the boundary condition
(
3.36
)
. We have

\displaystyle h_{\alpha}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},\xi)=w_{1}(\xi)\int_{\xi_{\star}}h_{\alpha}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},\xi_{\star})w_{2}(\xi_{\star})d\xi_{\star}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}w_{3}(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}r(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},\xi).

(3.71)

Now using
(
3.70
)
, we have

\displaystyle h_{\alpha}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},\xi_{\star})

\displaystyle=e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\nu_{M}}h_{\alpha,0}\left(x_{\mathbf{b}}-\frac{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\nu_{M}}K_{M}h_{\alpha}\left(s,x_{\mathbf{b}}-\frac{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}-s}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\nu_{M}}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}\left(s,x_{\mathbf{b}}-\frac{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}-s}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)ds.

Plugging the above into the last line of the equation
(
3.70
)
, we get
the term
\mathcal{I}_{2}
and
\mathcal{F}_{2}
in the decomposition of
\mathcal{I}
and
\mathcal{F}
.
The third term in the above corresponds to
\mathcal{M}_{2}
in the decomposition of
\mathcal{M}
. The proof is complete.
∎

Proposition 3.21
.

Recall
\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2}
in
(
3.64
)
,
(
3.65
)
, and
\mathcal{F},\mathcal{F}_{1}
,
\mathcal{F}_{2}
in
(
3.66
)
,
(
3.67
)
,
(
3.68
)
.
There hold

\displaystyle|\mathcal{M}_{1}(t)|+|\mathcal{M}_{2}(t)|

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|h_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|h_{\alpha}|_{t=0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\sup_{|\xi_{\star\star}|\lesssim 1}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\left\{e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi_{\star\star}}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|h_{\gamma}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}+\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\left\|w_{3}^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}r\right\|_{L^{\infty}},

and

\mathcal{F}(t)\lesssim\kappa\varepsilon^{2}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}.

Proof.

Using Lemma
3.1
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}\left\|h_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}+C_{\varepsilon_{0}}\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})|h_{\alpha}|\left(s,\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right)d\xi_{\star}ds

(3.72)

where
\widetilde{x}=x-\frac{t-s}{\varepsilon}\xi
.
Now using
(
3.70
)
again, we have

\displaystyle h_{\alpha}\left(s,\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right)

\displaystyle=1_{\left\{s<t_{\mathbf{b}}\left(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right)\right\}}e^{-\int_{0}^{s}\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\nu_{M}}h_{\alpha,0}\left(\widetilde{x}-\frac{s}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)

(3.73)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})\}}^{s}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\widetilde{s}}^{s}\nu_{M}}K_{M}h_{\alpha}\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle+\int_{\max\{0,s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})\}}^{s}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\widetilde{s}}^{s}\nu_{M}}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle+1_{\{s\geq t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})\}}e^{-\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int^{s}_{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}\left(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right)}\nu_{M}}h_{\alpha}\left(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}\left(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right),x_{\mathbf{b}}\left(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right),\xi_{\star}\right),

where

-\int_{\widetilde{s}}^{s}\nu_{M}=-\int_{\widetilde{s}}^{s}\nu_{M}(\tau,\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\tau}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star})d\tau.

Now we rewrite the last term in the above, using the boundary condition
(
3.36
)
.
Now we note that using the identity
(
3.36
)
for
x\to\widetilde{x}
and
\xi\to\xi_{\star}
and
t\to s
, the forth term in the above right hand side can be written as

\displaystyle h_{\alpha}(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),\xi_{\star})

\displaystyle=w_{1}(\xi_{\star})\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}h_{\alpha}(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),\xi_{\star\star})w_{2}(\xi_{\star\star})d\xi_{\star\star}

(3.74)

\displaystyle-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}w_{3}(\xi_{\star})^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}r(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),\xi_{\star}).

Combining
(
3.73
)
and
(
3.74
)
, we can bound
h_{\alpha}(s,\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})
as follows

\displaystyle|h_{\alpha}|(s,\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\|h_{\alpha,0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}+\varepsilon_{0}\left\|h_{\alpha,0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}

(3.75)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{0}^{s}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})|h_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star}d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}|\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle+w_{1}(\xi_{\star})\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}|h_{\alpha}|\left(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}\left(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right),x_{\mathbf{b}}\left(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right),\xi_{\star\star}\right)w_{2}(\xi_{\star\star})d\xi_{\star\star}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}w_{3}(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\partial^{\alpha}r|\left(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}\left(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right),x_{\mathbf{b}}\left(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}\right),\xi_{\star}\right).

Combining
(
3.72
)
and
(
3.75
)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{5}\mathcal{S}_{i},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\|h_{\alpha,0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}d\xi_{\star}ds,

(3.76)

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{0}^{s}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})|h_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star}d\widetilde{s}d\xi_{\star}ds,

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})\int_{0}^{s}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}|\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star}\right)d\widetilde{s}d\xi_{\star}ds,

(3.77)

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})1_{\{s\geq t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})\}}

\displaystyle\times e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\left\{w_{1}(\xi_{\star})\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}|h_{\alpha}|\left(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),\xi_{\star\star}\right)w_{2}(\xi_{\star\star})\right\}d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}ds,

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{5}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})\cdot\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}w_{3}(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\partial^{\alpha}r|\left(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),\xi_{\star}\right)d\xi_{\star}ds.

Treating
\mathcal{S}_{1}
.

We have

\mathcal{S}_{1}\lesssim\left\|h_{\alpha,0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}\cdot\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\frac{t-s}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}}ds\lesssim\left\|h_{\alpha,0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}.

Treating
\mathcal{S}_{2}
.
We have

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\kappa\varepsilon^{2})^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}

(3.78)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})|h_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}d\widetilde{s}ds.

We take
\delta_{1}>0
to be small and
\bar{N}>0
to be large. Define

U_{\bar{N}}=\left\{(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})\in\mathbb{R}^{6}:\quad\frac{1}{\bar{N}}<|\xi_{\star}-\xi_{\star\star}|<\bar{N},\quad|\xi_{\star}|<\bar{N}\right\}.

We decompose the integral
\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\xi_{\star}}\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}d\widetilde{s}
in
\mathcal{A}_{2}
as follows

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\xi_{\star}}\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle=\int_{|\widetilde{s}-s|\leq\delta_{1}\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}\int_{\widetilde{\xi}_{\star}}d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}d\widetilde{s}+\int_{|\widetilde{s}-s|\geq\delta_{1}\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{U_{\bar{N}}}d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle+\int_{|\widetilde{s}-s|\geq\delta_{1}\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{U_{\bar{N}}^{c}}d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}d\widetilde{s}ds.

and we have the corresponding decomposition

\mathcal{S}_{2}=\mathcal{S}_{2,1}+\mathcal{S}_{2,2}+\mathcal{S}_{2,3}.

Using the fact that

\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}}ds\lesssim 1,\quad\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}\int_{|\widetilde{s}-s|\leq\delta_{1}\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}d\widetilde{s}\lesssim\delta_{1},\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}k_{\rho}(\xi,\xi_{\star})k_{\rho}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}\lesssim 1,

we get the upper bound

\mathcal{S}_{2,1}\lesssim\delta_{1}\left\|h_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}.

As for
\mathcal{S}_{2,3}
, using the fact that, as
\bar{N}\to\infty
:

\int_{|\xi_{\star}|\geq\bar{N}}k_{\rho}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})d\xi_{\star\star}\lesssim\frac{1}{\bar{N}},\quad\sup_{\xi_{\star}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\{|\xi_{\star}-\xi_{\star\star}|\leq\frac{1}{\bar{N}}\}\cup\{|\xi_{\star}-\xi_{\star\star}|\geq\bar{N}\}}k_{\rho}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})d\xi_{\star\star}\to 0,

We have

\mathcal{S}_{2,3}\leq C_{\delta_{1}}c_{\bar{N}}\left\|h_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}.

where
c_{\bar{N}}\to 0
as
\bar{N}\to\infty
.
Finally for
\mathcal{A}_{2,2}
, first if
|\xi-\xi_{\star}|\geq\bar{N}
or
|\xi|\geq\bar{N}
, we have

\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})d\xi_{\star}\lesssim\frac{1}{\bar{N}},\qquad\sup_{\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|\xi-\xi_{\star}|\geq\bar{N}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})d\xi_{\star}\to 0\qquad\text{as}\quad\bar{N}\to\infty,

giving an upper bound
C_{\delta_{1}}c_{\bar{N}}\left\|h_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,\xi}}
.
We now consider
|\xi|\leq\bar{N}
and
|\xi-\xi_{\star}|\leq\bar{N}
. We have

\displaystyle\int_{\{\frac{1}{\bar{N}}<|\xi_{\star}-\xi_{\star\star}|<\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}|<\bar{N}\}}k_{\rho}(\xi,\xi_{\star})k_{\rho}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})|h_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\{|\xi_{\star\star}|<2\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}|<\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}-\xi_{\star\star}|>\bar{N}^{-1}\}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})|h_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\{|\xi_{\star\star}|<2\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}|<\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}-\xi_{\star\star}|>\bar{N}^{-1}\}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})

\displaystyle\left|\partial^{\alpha}\left(e^{\frac{|\xi_{\star\star}|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi_{\star\star}}f\right)\right|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\{|\xi_{\star\star}|<2\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}|<\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}-\xi_{\star\star}|>\bar{N}^{-1}\}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})|f_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\int_{\{|\xi_{\star\star}|<2\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}|<\bar{N},|\xi_{\star}-\xi_{\star\star}|>\bar{N}^{-1}\}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star})

\displaystyle\times\left|\partial^{\beta}\left\{e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi_{\star\star}}\right\}(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star})\right||f_{\gamma}|\left(\widetilde{s},\widetilde{x}-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{{\varepsilon}}\xi_{\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}(\widetilde{s})\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}(\widetilde{s})\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\sup_{|\xi_{\star\star}|\leq 2\bar{N}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\left\{e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi_{\star\star}}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|h_{\gamma}(\widetilde{s})\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}.

Here we use the change of variables

y=x-\frac{t-s}{\varepsilon}\xi-\frac{s-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star}
with

|dy|=|\frac{\varepsilon}{s-\widetilde{s}}|^{3}|d\xi_{\star}|\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{(\delta_{1}\kappa\varepsilon^{2})^{3}}|d\xi_{\star}|.

Hence we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{2,2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

(3.79)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\sup_{|\xi_{\star\star}|\leq 2\bar{N}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\left\{e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi_{\star\star}}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|h_{\gamma}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}.

Treating
\mathcal{S}_{3}
.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{S}_{3}
in
(
3.76
)
, we obtain

\mathcal{S}_{3}\lesssim\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}.

Treating
\mathcal{S}_{4}
.
We recall from
(
3.76
)
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})1_{\{s\geq t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})\}}

(3.80)

\displaystyle\times e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\left\{w_{1}(\xi_{\star})\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}|h_{\alpha}|\left(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),\xi_{\star\star}\right)w_{2}(\xi_{\star\star})\right\}d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}ds.

Now using
(
3.70
)
, we get

\displaystyle|h_{\alpha}|(s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star}),\xi_{\star\star})

(3.81)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|h_{\alpha,0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star\star}\rangle\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}|K_{M}h_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X,\xi_{\star})}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star\star}\rangle\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X,\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}|\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\widetilde{s}.

Combining
(
3.80
)
and
(
3.81
)
, we get
