\mathcal{S}_{4}\lesssim\left\|h_{\alpha,0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}+\mathcal{S}_{4,1}+\mathcal{S}_{4,2},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{4,1}=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{4\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}}w_{1}(\xi_{\star})\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}

(3.82)

\displaystyle\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}\int_{0}^{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X,\xi_{\star})}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star\star}\rangle\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X,\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}|K_{M}h_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle\times w_{2}(\xi_{\star\star})d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}ds

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{4,2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}}w_{1}(\xi_{\star}),

\displaystyle\times\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}\int_{0}^{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star\star}\rangle\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}|\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)d\widetilde{s}

\displaystyle\times w_{2}(\xi_{\star\star})d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}ds.

We bound
\mathcal{S}_{4,1}
. Using Lemma
3.1
, we get

\displaystyle K_{M}h_{\alpha}\left(\widetilde{s},x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}\langle\xi_{\star\star}\rangle\left\|h_{\alpha}(\widetilde{s})\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\xi_{\star\star\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star\star})|h_{\alpha}|\left(\widetilde{s},x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star\star}\right)d\xi_{\star\star\star}.

Combining the above with the definition of
\mathcal{A}_{4,2}
in
(
3.82
)
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{4,1}\lesssim\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}\}}^{t}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi\rangle\frac{t-s}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}\int_{\xi_{\star}}\int_{\xi_{\star\star}}k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi,\xi_{\star})k_{\rho_{0}}(\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star\star})e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star}\rangle\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}}w_{1}(\xi_{\star})

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{\xi_{\star\star\star}}\int_{0}^{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X,\xi_{\star})}e^{-c_{0}\langle\xi_{\star\star}\rangle\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(X,\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}}h_{\alpha}\left(\widetilde{s},x_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\frac{s-t_{\mathbf{b}}(\widetilde{x},\xi_{\star})-\widetilde{s}}{\varepsilon}\xi_{\star\star},\xi_{\star\star\star}\right)

\displaystyle\times w_{2}(\xi_{\star\star})d\xi_{\star\star\star}d\xi_{\star\star}d\xi_{\star}d\widetilde{s}ds,\qquad\widetilde{x}=x-\frac{t-s}{\varepsilon}\xi.

Similarly as in the estimate of the term
\mathcal{S}_{2}
in
(
3.78
)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{4,1}\lesssim o(1)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|h_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\sup_{|\xi_{\star\star}|\leq 2\bar{N}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\left\{e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi_{\star\star}}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|h_{\gamma}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}.

As for the term
\mathcal{S}_{4,2}
, we get

\mathcal{S}_{4,3}\lesssim\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}.

Treating
\mathcal{S}_{5}
.
Recalling
\mathcal{S}_{5}
in
(
3.76
)
, we have

\mathcal{S}_{5}\lesssim\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\left\|w_{3}^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}r\right\|_{L^{\infty}}.

The bounds for
\mathcal{M}_{2}
is similar and we skip the details. The proof is complete.
∎

Combining the propositions below, we have

Theorem 3.22
.

Let
h_{\alpha}
solve
(
3.35
)
with the boundary condition
(
3.36
)
. Recall the analytic norms in
(
3.7
)
. There holds

\displaystyle\left\|h_{\alpha}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|h_{\alpha,0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\sup_{|\xi_{\star\star}|\lesssim 1}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\left\{e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi_{\star\star}}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|h_{\gamma}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}(s)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\left\|w_{3}^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}r\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}},

and we have the analytic estimate

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{h}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{H}_{h}(0)+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{6}_{x})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{2}_{x,\xi})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}\left\|\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\left\|\left\|w_{3}^{-1}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}r\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{\infty}_{x,\xi})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

We now recall from
(
3.35
)
that

\widetilde{g}_{\alpha}=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}[L_{M},\partial^{\alpha}]h+\partial^{\alpha}\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta\sqrt{\mu}_{M}}\textbf{R}_{a}+\frac{\delta}{\kappa{\varepsilon}}\Gamma_{M}(h,h)+\frac{2}{\kappa}\frac{Q({\bf{f_{2}}}\sqrt{\mu},h\sqrt{\mu_{M}})}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}\right).

Definition 3.23
.

We define the following quantity

\displaystyle d_{\infty}(t)=\frac{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}{\delta}\left\|\textbf{NS}(U,P)\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\frac{\kappa{\varepsilon}}{\delta}\left\|{\rm div}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\frac{{\varepsilon}^{3}}{\delta}(\||P\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\kappa\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty})^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa}{\delta}\left\{\||\partial_{t}P+U\cdot\nabla_{x}P\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\varepsilon\||P(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\||P\partial_{i}U_{j}\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\left(\||(\partial_{t}+U\cdot\nabla_{x})(\partial_{i}U_{j})\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\varepsilon\||(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)(\partial_{i}U_{j})\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\||\nabla_{x}U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\kappa^{2}\varepsilon^{3}}{\delta}\||\partial_{i}U_{j}\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\||U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\left\|\nabla_{x}^{2}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}P\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\sum_{j=1}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{3}}U_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\right\}.

We have
d_{\infty}(t)\lesssim\varepsilon^{0.99}
for suitable fluid solutions.
Next we bound each term in the above. Next, we show the bound for
[L_{M},\partial^{\alpha}]
.

Lemma 3.24
.

There holds

\left\|\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}[L_{M},\partial^{\alpha}]h\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{\infty}_{x,\xi})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{H}_{h}(t),

and

\frac{\delta}{\kappa\varepsilon}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{M}(h,h)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{\infty}_{x,\xi})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\delta}{\kappa\varepsilon}\mathcal{H}_{h}(t)^{2}.

(3.83)

Proof.

From
(
3.16
)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}L_{M}h

\displaystyle=\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu_{M}(\xi_{\star})}h(\xi_{\star})d\xi_{\star}d{\omega}

\displaystyle+h(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu_{M}(\xi_{\star})}\left(\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}(\xi^{\prime})h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+h(\xi^{\prime})\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}.

Since
[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f=0
for
|\alpha|=0
, we only need to consider
|\alpha|\geq 1
.
We have

\displaystyle h

\displaystyle=2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu_{M}(\xi_{\star})}\partial^{\beta}\left\{\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}\right\}(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\gamma}h(\xi^{\prime}_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu_{M}(\xi_{\star})}\partial^{\beta}\left\{\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}\right\}(\xi^{\prime}_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}h(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\gamma}h(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\beta}\left\{\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}\right\}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu_{M}(\xi_{\star})}\partial^{\gamma}h(\xi_{\star})d\xi_{\star}d{\omega}.

By a direct calculation, we get

\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}}}=T_{M}^{3/4}e^{-\frac{1}{2}|\xi-\varepsilon U|^{2}+\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}}=T_{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{1}{4}|\xi|^{2}+\varepsilon U\cdot\xi-\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}.

(3.84)

Hence we get

\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}[L_{M},\partial^{\alpha}]h\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}h\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial^{\beta}\mu}{\sqrt{\mu}_{M}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|\langle\xi\rangle\partial^{\beta}\mu\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}\right\}.

Using Proposition
(
2.3
)
and the discrete Young inequality, we have the result.
Next we show
(
3.84
)
. We have

\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{M}(h,h)=\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\xi\rangle^{-1}\Gamma_{M}(h_{\beta},h_{\gamma})\lesssim\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|h_{\beta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|h_{\gamma}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}.

Using the discrete Young inequality and
(
3.54
)
, we obtain
(
3.84
)
.
∎

Proposition 3.25
.

Recalling
\textbf{R}_{a}
in
(
3.27
)
, there holds

\kappa\varepsilon^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta}\left\|\left\|\frac{1}{\nu}\partial^{\alpha}\left(\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\textbf{R}_{a}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim d_{\infty}(t).

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\varepsilon\delta}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\textbf{R}_{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}{\delta}\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}-\frac{{\varepsilon}^{3}}{\delta}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},{\bf{f_{2}}})+\frac{\kappa{\varepsilon}}{3\delta}{\rm div}U|\varphi|^{2}\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\partial_{i\ell}U_{j}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})(\varphi_{\ell}A_{ij})\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa}{\delta}\cdot\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{(\partial_{t}P+U\cdot\nabla_{x}P)+\varepsilon P(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi+\frac{1}{2}P\partial_{i}U_{\ell}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{\ell}\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{\varepsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{(\partial_{t}+U\cdot\nabla_{x})(\partial_{i}U_{j})+\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi\partial_{i}U_{j}+\partial_{i}U_{j}\partial_{k}U_{\ell}\varphi_{k}\varphi_{\ell}\right\}A_{ij}

\displaystyle-\frac{\kappa^{2}\varepsilon^{3}}{\delta}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{i}U_{j}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}+(\varepsilon^{2}U+\varphi)\cdot\nabla_{x}\right)A_{ij}.

We note that

\begin{cases}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}&=T_{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}e^{\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}},\\
\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}&=T_{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(2\pi)^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{1}{4}(2-T_{M}^{-1})|\xi|^{2}}e^{-\frac{1}{2T_{M}}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}e^{\frac{\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}{4T_{M}}}.\end{cases}

(3.85)

First we bound

\frac{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}{\delta}\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}=T_{M}^{-\frac{3}{2}}(2\pi)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\frac{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}{\delta}\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi e^{-\frac{1}{4}(2-T_{M}^{-1})|\xi|^{2}}e^{-\frac{1}{2T_{M}}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}e^{\frac{\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}{4T_{M}}}.

Using the product rule and Lemma
5.5
and Lemma
5.4
for the function
e^{-\frac{1}{4}(2-T_{M}^{-1})|\xi|^{2}}e^{-\frac{1}{2T_{M}}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}
, we get

\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{3}\textbf{NS}(U,P)\cdot\varphi\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}{\delta}\left\|\textbf{NS}(U,P)\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}e^{-\frac{1}{10}(2-T_{M}^{-1})|\xi|^{2}}.

Using Proposition
5.13
and recalling
(
3.11
)
, we have, for
|T^{-1}_{M}-1|\ll 1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left|e^{\frac{1}{10}|\xi|^{2}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},{\bf{f_{2}}})\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|e^{o(1)|T_{M}^{-1}-1||\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf{f_{2}}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\left\{\||P\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\kappa\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right\}^{2}.

Hence

\frac{{\varepsilon}^{3}}{\delta}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},{\bf{f_{2}}})\right\}\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{{\varepsilon}^{3}}{\delta}e^{-\frac{1}{10}|\xi|^{2}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}\left\{\||P\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\kappa\left\|\nabla_{x}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right\}^{2}.

Now we bound

\frac{\kappa{\varepsilon}}{3\delta}{\rm div}U|\varphi|^{2}\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{1}{3}T_{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}(2\pi)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\kappa{\varepsilon}}{\delta}{\rm div}U|\varphi|^{2}e^{-\frac{1}{4}(2-T_{M}^{-1})|\xi|^{2}}e^{-\frac{1}{2T_{M}}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}e^{\frac{\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}{4T_{M}}}.

Similarly as above, using Lemma
5.4
and Lemma
5.5
for the function
e^{-\frac{1}{4}(2-T_{M}^{-1})|\xi|^{2}}e^{-\frac{1}{2T_{M}}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}
, we have

\frac{\kappa{\varepsilon}}{\delta}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{{\rm div}U|\varphi|^{2}\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\kappa{\varepsilon}}{\delta}\left\|{\rm div}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}e^{-\frac{1}{10}(2-T_{M}^{-1})|\xi|^{2}}.

Next we bound

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\partial_{i\ell}U_{j}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})(\varphi_{\ell}A_{ij})\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=T_{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\partial_{i\ell}U_{j}e^{\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})(\varphi_{\ell}A_{ij}(\varphi)).

Since the function
e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}
is analytic, it suffices to bound

\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left|e^{\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}\partial^{\alpha}\left\{e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}\partial_{i\ell}U_{j}\varphi_{\ell}A_{ij}\right\}\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

As in the estimate
(
5.32
)
and the product rule, we have, for
|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\ll 1
:

\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left|e^{\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}\partial^{\alpha}\left\{e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}\partial_{i\ell}U_{j}\varphi_{\ell}A_{ij}\right\}\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\left\|\nabla_{x}^{2}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},\infty}e^{-\frac{1}{10}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)|\xi|^{2}}.

Here we use the fact that
A_{ij}
is analytic decay exponentially, proven in Corollary
5.24
.
Next, we consider

\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa}{\delta}\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{(\partial_{t}P+U\cdot\nabla_{x}P)+\varepsilon P(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi+\frac{1}{2}P\partial_{i}U_{\ell}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{\ell}\right\}.

Similarly, recalling
(
3.85
)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa}{\delta}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left|\partial^{\alpha}\left(\mu\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{(\partial_{t}P+U\cdot\nabla_{x}P)+\varepsilon P(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi+\frac{1}{2}P\partial_{i}U_{\ell}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{\ell}\right\}\right)\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{2}\kappa}{\delta}\left\{\||\partial_{t}P+U\cdot\nabla_{x}P\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\varepsilon\||P(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\||P\partial_{i}U_{j}\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right\}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{1}{10}(2-T_{M}^{-1})|\xi|^{2}}.

Next we bound

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{(\partial_{t}+U\cdot\nabla_{x})(\partial_{i}U_{j})+\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi\partial_{i}U_{j}+\partial_{i}U_{j}\partial_{k}U_{\ell}\varphi_{k}\varphi_{\ell}\right\}A_{ij}

\displaystyle=T_{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\varepsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}e^{\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}A_{ij}

\displaystyle\times e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}\left\{(\partial_{t}+U\cdot\nabla_{x})(\partial_{i}U_{j})+\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi\partial_{i}U_{j}+\partial_{i}U_{j}\partial_{k}U_{\ell}\varphi_{k}\varphi_{\ell}\right\},

where we recall
(
3.85
)
. Similarly, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{(\partial_{t}+U\cdot\nabla_{x})(\partial_{i}U_{j})+\varepsilon(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)\cdot\varphi\partial_{i}U_{j}+\partial_{i}U_{j}\partial_{k}U_{\ell}\varphi_{k}\varphi_{\ell}\right\}A_{ij}\right\}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}\kappa^{2}}{\delta}e^{-\frac{1}{10}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)|\xi|^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\||(\partial_{t}+U\cdot\nabla_{x})(\partial_{i}U_{j})\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\varepsilon\||(\partial_{t}U+U\cdot\nabla_{x}U)(\partial_{i}U_{j})\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}+\||\nabla_{x}U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}^{2}\right).

Next we bound the analytic norm of

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa^{2}\varepsilon^{3}}{\delta}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{i}U_{j}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}+(\varepsilon^{2}U+\varphi)\cdot\nabla_{x}\right)A_{ij}

\displaystyle=T_{M}^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}\varepsilon^{3}}{\delta}e^{\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)}e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}\partial_{i}U_{j}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}+(\varepsilon^{2}U+\varphi)\cdot\nabla_{x}\right)A_{ij}.

Using Corollary
5.24
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa^{2}\varepsilon^{3}}{\delta}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{i}U_{j}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}+(\varepsilon^{2}U+\varphi)\cdot\nabla_{x}\right)A_{ij}\right\}\right|\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\varepsilon^{3}}{\delta}\||\partial_{i}U_{j}\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\left(1+\varepsilon^{2}\||U\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\right)e^{-\frac{1}{10}(T_{M}^{-1}-1)|\xi|^{2}}.

The proof is complete.∎

Lemma 3.26
.

Recalling
r
in
(
3.25
)
and
(
3.11
)
. There holds

\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\left\|\left\|w_{3}^{-1}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}r\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{\infty}_{x,\xi})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}P\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\sum_{j=1}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{3}}U_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\right\}.

Proof.

We have

{\bf{f_{2}}}|_{x_{3}=0}=P|_{x_{3}=0}\sqrt{\mu}_{0}-\sum_{j=1}^{2}\partial_{x_{3}}U_{j}|_{x_{3}=0}A_{3j}(\xi),

and

\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}{\bf{f_{2}}}=c_{\mu}\sqrt{\mu}_{0}\left\{P|_{x_{3}=0}\int_{\xi_{3}<0}\mu_{0}(\xi)|\xi_{3}|d\xi-\sum_{j=1}^{2}\partial_{x_{3}}U_{j}|_{x_{3}=0}\int_{\xi_{3}<0}A_{3j}(\xi)\sqrt{\mu}_{0}(\xi)|\xi_{3}|d\xi\right\}.

This implies

\partial^{\alpha}r\lesssim\left(|\partial^{\alpha}P(t,x_{1},x_{2},0)|+\sum_{j=1}^{2}|\partial_{x_{3}}\partial^{\alpha}U_{j}(t,x_{1},x_{2},0)|\right)\sqrt{\mu}_{0}.

The result follows.
∎

Combining all of the above estimates, we obtain the theorem:

Theorem 3.27
.

Let
f
solve
(
3.23
)
with the boundary condition
(
3.24
)
. There holds

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{H}_{h}|_{t=0}+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{6}_{x})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle+(\varepsilon\kappa)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{2}_{x,\xi})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{0.99}.

3.9
Analytic
L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}
estimates

In this section we derive the
L^{6}
analytic estimate for

\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t,L^{6}_{x})}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}},

where

f
solves
(
3.23
)
with the boundary condition
(
3.24
)
.
We recall the equation
(
3.32
)
with the boundary condition
(
3.34
)
.
We have

\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{{\varepsilon}}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}Lf_{\alpha}=G_{\alpha},

where

\displaystyle G_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f-\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f\right\}

(3.86)

\displaystyle+\partial^{\alpha}\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta\sqrt{\mu}}\textbf{R}_{a}+\frac{\delta}{\kappa\varepsilon}\Gamma(f,f)+\frac{2}{\kappa}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},f)\right),

along with the boundary condition

f_{\alpha}|_{\gamma_{-}}=\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}r.

We recall

\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}=a_{\alpha}(t,x)\sqrt{\mu}+b_{\alpha}(t,x)\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu}+c_{\alpha}(t,x)\frac{|\varphi|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}.

We have, for any test function
\psi
:

\displaystyle\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi,\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}-\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle=-\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi,({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\langle\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

(3.87)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon}\langle L\psi,({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}-\langle\varepsilon G_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

where

\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}=-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{2}}\xi_{3}\left(f_{\alpha}\psi\right)|_{x_{3}=0}dx_{1}dx_{2}d\xi.

Proposition 3.28
.

There holds

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{\frac{1}{3}}+\left\|\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+(\kappa{\varepsilon})^{-1}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\|\partial^{\alpha}r\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma_{-}}}+\varepsilon\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-9}G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

Proof.

We will estimate
\left\|a_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}},\left\|b_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}}
and
\left\|c_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}}
respectively.

Estimating
\left\|a_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}}
and
\left\|c_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}

:

We choose the test function
\psi
to be

\begin{cases}\psi_{a}(t,x,\xi)&=(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta_{a})\sqrt{\mu}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\varphi_{j}\partial_{x_{j}}\phi_{a},\qquad\beta_{a}=10,\\
\psi_{c}(t,x,\xi)&=(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta_{c})\sqrt{\mu}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\varphi_{j}\partial_{x_{j}}\phi_{c},\qquad\beta_{c}=5,\end{cases}

where
\phi_{a}=\phi_{a}(t,x),\phi_{c}=\phi_{c}(t,x)
solve the elliptic equation

\begin{cases}&-\Delta\phi_{a}=a_{\alpha}^{5},\quad\partial_{x_{3}}\phi_{a}|_{x_{3}=0}=0,\\
&-\Delta\phi_{c}=c_{\alpha}^{5},\quad\phi_{c}|_{x_{3}=0}=0.\\
\end{cases}

(3.88)

It is standard that
\phi\in\{\phi_{a},\phi_{c}\}
satisfy the following standard elliptic estimates

\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi\right\|_{L^{4/3}(\partial\Omega)}+\left\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi\right\|_{L^{6/5}(\Omega)}+\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi\right\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\phi\right\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}\lesssim\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}^{5}.

(3.89)

Now we have, for
\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}
and
\beta\in\{\beta_{a},\beta_{c}\}
, we have

\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi=\sum_{i,j}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{j}(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{i}x_{j}}\phi+R_{\phi},

where

R_{\phi}=\sum_{i,j}\xi_{i}\partial_{x_{j}}\left((|\varphi|^{2}-\beta)\sqrt{\mu}\varphi_{j}\right)\partial_{x_{i}}\phi+\varepsilon\sum_{i,j}U_{i}\left\{\varphi_{j}(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta)\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\partial_{x_{i}x_{j}}\phi.

This implies, for
\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}
:

\displaystyle\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi,\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3}\left\{\langle|\varphi|^{2}(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta),\mu\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}\right\}\langle a_{\alpha},\Delta\phi\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

(3.90)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}\langle|\varphi|^{2}(|\varphi|^{2}-3)(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta),\mu\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}\langle c_{\alpha},\Delta\phi\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\langle R_{\phi},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

Combining the above with
(
3.88
)
,

(
3.87
)
and the fact that

\begin{cases}&\langle|\varphi|^{2}(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta_{a}),\mu\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}\neq 0,\qquad\langle|\varphi|^{2}(|\varphi|^{2}-3)(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta_{a}),\mu\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}=0,\\
&\langle|\varphi|^{2}(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta_{c}),\mu\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}=0,\qquad\langle|\varphi|^{2}(|\varphi|^{2}-3)(|\varphi|^{2}-\beta_{c}),\mu\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}\neq 0,\\
\end{cases}

we get

\displaystyle\left\|a_{\alpha}(t)\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{6}+\left\|c_{\alpha}(t)\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{6}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}}\left\{\left|\langle R_{\phi},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|+\left|\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right|+\left|\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi,({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|+\langle\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\max_{\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}}\left\{\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon}\left|\langle L\psi,({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|+\left|\langle\varepsilon G_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\max_{\phi\in\{\phi_{a},\phi_{c}\}}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\max_{\phi\in\{\phi_{a},\phi_{c}\}}\left\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi\right\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\max_{\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}}\left|\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right|

\displaystyle+\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x,\xi}}\max_{\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}}\left\|\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\right\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\left\{\left\|\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\varepsilon G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\}\max_{\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}}\left\|\psi\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\max_{\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}}\left\|L\psi\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}+o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{6}+\max_{\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}}\left|\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right|+\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi\right\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\left\{\left\|\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\varepsilon G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+(\kappa{\varepsilon})^{-1}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\}\max_{\phi\in\{\phi_{a},\phi_{c}\}}\left\|\nabla_{x}\phi\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}+o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{6}+\max_{\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}}\left|\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right|

\displaystyle+\left\{\left\|\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\varepsilon G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+(\kappa{\varepsilon})^{-1}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x,\xi}}\right\}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5},

we used
(
3.89
)
. We shall now treat the boundary term

\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}=\int_{\partial\Omega\times\mathbb{R}_{\xi}^{3}}f(t,x,v)\psi(t,x)(\xi\cdot n(x))dS(x)d\xi.

To this end, we decompose

\displaystyle f_{\alpha}|_{\gamma}=1_{\gamma_{+}}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma})f_{\alpha}+\mathbf{P}_{\gamma}f_{\alpha}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}1_{\gamma_{-}}\partial^{\alpha}r.

We recall

\mathbf{P}_{\gamma}f_{\alpha}=c_{\mu}\sqrt{\mu}_{0}(\xi)\left\{\int_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}(t,x,\widetilde{\xi})\sqrt{\mu}_{0}(\widetilde{\xi})(n(x)\cdot\widetilde{\xi})d\widetilde{\xi}\right\}.

Hence we have, for
\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}
and
\beta\in\{\beta_{a},\beta_{c}\}

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{P}_{\gamma}f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}

(3.91)

\displaystyle=c_{\mu}\int_{\partial\Omega\times\mathbb{R}_{\xi}^{3}}\left\{\int_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}(t,x,\widetilde{\xi})\sqrt{\mu}_{0}(\widetilde{\xi})(n(x)\cdot\widetilde{\xi})d\widetilde{\xi}\right\}{\mu_{0}}(\xi)(|\xi|^{2}-\beta)\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi(\xi\cdot n(x))d\xi dS(x)

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}_{\xi}^{3}}\left\{\int_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}(t,x,\widetilde{\xi})\sqrt{\mu}_{0}(\widetilde{\xi})(n(x)\cdot\widetilde{\xi})d\widetilde{\xi}\right\}\mu_{0}(\xi)(|\xi|^{2}-\beta)(\xi_{\parallel}\partial_{x_{\parallel}}\phi+\xi_{3}\partial_{x_{3}}\phi)(-\xi_{3})d\xi dx_{\parallel}

\displaystyle=0.

Here we used the fact that
\partial_{x_{3}}\phi_{a}=0
on
\partial\Omega
and

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\xi_{\parallel}\xi_{3}(|\xi|^{2}-\beta)\mu_{0}(\xi)d\xi=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\xi_{3}^{2}(|\xi|^{2}-\beta_{c})\mu_{0}(\xi)d\xi=0.

This implies, for
\psi\in\{\psi_{a},\psi_{c}\}
:

\displaystyle\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle=\langle 1_{\gamma_{+}}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma})f_{\alpha}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}1_{\gamma_{-}}\partial^{\alpha}r,\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma_{+}}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi\|_{L^{4/3}(\partial\Omega)}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\|\partial^{\alpha}r\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma_{-}}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi\|_{L^{4/3}(\partial\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}\left(\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma})f_{\alpha}\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\|\partial^{\alpha}r\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma_{-}}}\right),

where we used
(
3.89
)
.

Estimating
\left\|b_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}
:

To bound
b_{\alpha}=(b_{\alpha,1},b_{\alpha,2},b_{\alpha,3})
, we first choose the test function, for
i,j\in\{1,2,3\}

\psi=\psi_{b}^{i,j}=(\varphi_{i}^{2}-1)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{j}\phi_{b}^{j},\qquad-\Delta\phi_{b}^{j}=b_{\alpha,j}^{5},\quad\phi_{b}^{j}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.

Then we have the elliptic estimate

\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{b}^{j}\|_{L^{4/3}(\partial\Omega)}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi_{b}^{j}\|_{L^{6/5}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{b}^{j}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\phi_{b}^{j}\|_{L^{6}}\lesssim\left\|Pf_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}(\Omega)}^{5}.

We have

\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi_{b}^{i,j}=\varphi_{k}(\varphi_{i}^{2}-1)\sqrt{\mu}\cdot\partial_{x_{j}x_{k}}\phi_{b}^{j}+R_{\phi_{b}^{j}},

where

R_{\phi_{b}^{j}}=\xi_{k}\partial_{x_{k}}\left((\varphi_{i}^{2}-1)\sqrt{\mu}\right)\partial_{x_{j}}\phi_{b}^{j}+\varepsilon U_{k}(\varphi_{i}^{2}-1)\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{x_{j}x_{k}}\phi_{b}^{j}.

This implies

\displaystyle\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi_{b}^{i,j},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{3}\langle\varphi_{k}^{2}(\varphi_{i}^{2}-1),\mu\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}\langle b_{\alpha,k},\partial_{x_{j}x_{k}}\phi_{b}^{j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\langle R_{\phi_{b}^{j}},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

(3.92)

\displaystyle=-2\langle b_{\alpha,i},\partial_{ij}\phi_{b}^{j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\langle R_{\phi_{b}^{j}},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

where we used the fact that

\langle\varphi_{k}^{2}(\varphi_{i}^{2}-1),\mu\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}=\begin{cases}&-2\qquad\text{if}\quad i=k\\
&0\qquad\text{if}\quad i\neq k.\end{cases}

Combining
(
3.92
)
with
(
3.87
)
, we get

\displaystyle\langle\partial_{ij}(-\Delta_{D})^{-1}b_{\alpha,j},b_{\alpha,i}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\langle R_{\phi_{b}^{j}},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|+\left|\langle f_{\alpha},\psi_{b}^{i,j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right|+\left|\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi_{b}^{i,j},({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|+\langle\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha},\psi_{b}^{i,j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon}\left|\langle L\psi_{b}^{i,j},({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|+\left|\langle\varepsilon G_{\alpha},\psi_{b}^{i,j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}+o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{6}+\left|\langle f_{\alpha},\psi_{b}^{i,j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right|

\displaystyle+\left\{\left\|\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\varepsilon G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+(\kappa{\varepsilon})^{-1}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x,\xi}}\right\}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}.

For the boundary term, as in the above, it suffices to see that

\langle\mathbf{P}_{\gamma}f_{\alpha},(\xi_{i}^{2}-1)\sqrt{\mu}_{0}\partial_{j}\phi_{b}^{j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}=0.

To this end, using a similar argument as in
(
3.91
)
, we get

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{P}_{\gamma}f_{\alpha},(\xi_{i}^{2}-1)\sqrt{\mu}_{0}\partial_{j}\phi_{b}^{j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle=-c_{\mu}\int_{\partial\Omega\times\mathbb{R}_{\xi}^{3}}\left\{\int_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}(t,x,\widetilde{\xi})\sqrt{\mu}_{0}(\widetilde{\xi})(n(x)\cdot\widetilde{\xi})d\widetilde{\xi}\right\}{\mu_{0}}(\xi)\xi_{3}(\xi_{i}^{2}-1)\partial_{j}\phi_{b}^{j}d\xi dS(x)=0.

Finally, we get

\displaystyle\left|\langle\partial_{ij}(-\Delta_{D})^{-1}\left(b_{\alpha,j}^{5}\right),b_{\alpha,i}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\right|

(3.93)

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}+o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{6}+\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}\left(\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma})f_{\alpha}\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma_{+}}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\|\partial^{\alpha}r\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma_{-}}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\{\left\|\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\varepsilon G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+(\kappa{\varepsilon})^{-1}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x,\xi}}\right\}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}.

Next, for
i\neq j
, we choose the test function to be

\psi=|\varphi|^{2}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{j}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{j}\phi_{b}^{j}.

We have

\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi=\sum_{k=1}^{3}\varphi_{k}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{j}|\varphi|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{jk}\phi_{b}^{j}+R_{\psi}^{i,j},

where

R_{\psi}^{i,j}=\sum_{k=1}^{3}\xi_{k}\partial_{x_{k}}\left(\varphi_{i}\varphi_{j}|\varphi|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}\right)\partial_{j}\phi_{b}^{j}+\varepsilon\sum_{k=1}^{3}U_{k}\varphi_{i}\varphi_{j}|\varphi|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}\partial_{jk}\phi_{b}^{j}.

This implies

\displaystyle\langle\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi,\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k,m}\langle\varphi_{i}\varphi_{j}\sqrt{\mu},\varphi_{k}\varphi_{m}\sqrt{\mu}\rangle_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}\langle b_{\alpha,m},\partial_{jk}\phi_{b}^{j}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\langle R_{\psi}^{i,j},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k,m}(\delta_{ij}\delta_{km}+\delta_{ik}\delta_{jm}+\delta_{im}\delta_{jk})\langle\partial_{jk}\phi_{b}^{j},b_{\alpha,m}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\langle R_{\psi}^{i,j},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\langle\partial_{ij}\phi_{b}^{j},b_{j,\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\langle\partial_{j}^{2}\phi^{j}_{b},b_{\alpha,i}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\langle R_{\psi}^{i,j},\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

where we use the fact that
i\neq j
. Combining the above with
(
3.87
)
, we have

\displaystyle 1_{j\neq i}\left|\langle\partial_{j}^{2}\Delta_{D}^{-1}\left(b_{\alpha,j}^{5}\right),b_{i,\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\right|

(3.94)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\langle\partial_{ij}\Delta_{D}^{-1}\left(b_{i,\alpha}^{5}\right),b_{i,\alpha}\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}\right|+o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}+o(1)\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{6}+\left|\langle f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right|

\displaystyle+\left\{\left\|\varepsilon\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\varepsilon G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+(\kappa{\varepsilon})^{-1}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x,\xi}}\right\}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{5}.

Here we used the fact that

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{P}_{\gamma}f_{\alpha},\psi\rangle_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}=-c_{\mu}\int_{\partial\Omega\times\mathbb{R}_{\xi}^{3}}\left\{\int_{\gamma_{+}}f_{\alpha}(t,x,\widetilde{\xi})\sqrt{\mu}_{0}(\widetilde{\xi})(n(x)\cdot\widetilde{\xi})d\widetilde{\xi}\right\}{\mu_{0}}(\xi)|\xi|^{2}\xi_{3}\xi_{i}\xi_{j}\partial_{j}\phi_{b}^{j}d\xi dS(x)=0.

Combining
(
3.94
)
and
(
3.93
)
, we get the result. Finally, we note that

\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x,\xi}}\lesssim\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{\frac{1}{3}},

and

\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{4}_{\gamma_{+}}}\lesssim\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\xi}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

We now focus on estimating
\left\|\varepsilon\langle\xi\rangle^{-9}G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}
where
G_{\alpha}
is given in
(
3.86
)
.

Lemma 3.29
.

There holds

\displaystyle\varepsilon\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-9}G_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{0.99}+\varepsilon\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)+\delta\varepsilon\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}.

Proof.

First, we get

\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}}\left\|\left\|\langle\xi\rangle^{-2}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{f}(t),

where we used
(
3.11
)
.
Next we use Lemma
3.12
to get

\varepsilon\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f\right\}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{f}(t).

Next we have

\displaystyle\frac{\delta}{\kappa}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,f)\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\varepsilon\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell^{2}_{\alpha}}.

Finally for
{\bf{R}}_{a}
term, we bound exactly as in Proposition
3.17
.
The proof is complete.
∎

From the Proposition
3.28
and the above analytic bound on the forcing term, we obtain the following theorem

Theorem 3.30
.

Let
f
solve
(
3.23
)
. There holds

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\kappa^{\frac{1}{6}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}^{\frac{1}{3}}+\left\|\mathcal{Y}_{f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\right\}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{0.99}+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}+\delta\varepsilon\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\right\}.

3.10
Analytic
L^{2}_{t}L^{p}_{x}
estimate for
p\in(2,3)

In this section, we estimate

\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}},

appearing in the bound of the nonlinear term in Proposition
3.16
.
To this end, we recall the equation for
f_{\alpha}
in
(
3.32
)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}Lf_{\alpha}+\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f+g_{\alpha}+\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f_{\alpha}-\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f\right\},

where

g_{\alpha}=\partial^{\alpha}\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta\sqrt{\mu}}\textbf{R}_{a}+\frac{\delta}{\kappa\varepsilon}\Gamma(f,f)+\frac{2}{\kappa}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},f)\right),

along with the boundary condition

f_{\alpha}|_{\gamma_{-}}=\mathbf{P}_{\gamma}f_{\alpha}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\partial^{\alpha}r,

where
r
is given in
(
3.25
)
.
We recall the following Proposition, which the averaging in
\xi
lemma for the transport equation with boundaries, proved in
[
48
]
:

Proposition 3.31
.

Let
p\in(2,3)
. There holds

\left\|\mathbf{P}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\left\|f_{0}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}+\left\|f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|(\varepsilon\partial_{t}+\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})f\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}.

Theorem 3.32
.

Let
p\in(2,3)
and
f
solves the equation
(
3.23
)
, there holds

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{p}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{0}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}+\varepsilon^{0.99}

\displaystyle+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

Proof.

Applying Proposition
3.31
for
f_{\alpha}
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{0}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}+\left\|f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+(\kappa\varepsilon)^{-1}\left\{\left\|Lf_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}+\left\|[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\}+\delta^{-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\bf{R}}_{a}\right\}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x\xi}}

\displaystyle+\delta\kappa^{-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,f)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},f)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}+\varepsilon\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}f\right\}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}.

As in the
L^{2}
analytic estimates, we obtain

\displaystyle(\kappa{\varepsilon})^{-1}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\varepsilon\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,f)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\varepsilon\kappa^{-1}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},f)\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{0.1}\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+o(1)\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}+\varepsilon^{-1}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu})f\right\}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\varepsilon^{2}\kappa\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

4
Proof of the main theorem

Fix
p\in(2,3)
and denote
\lambda\in(0,1)
such that
\frac{1}{3}=\frac{\lambda}{6}+\frac{1-\lambda}{p}
. By the interpolation and the discrete Holder inequality, we have

\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{\lambda}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{p}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{1-\lambda}.

We define the quantity

\zeta_{f}(t)=\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{p}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

From the above inequality, we see that
\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\zeta_{f}(t)
.
From Theorem
3.15
and Proposition
3.16
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\right)\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\delta\varepsilon\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(s)

\displaystyle+\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}){\bf{f_{2}}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{\gamma_{-}}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta}\left\||{\rm div}U\right\||_{\tau_{0},2}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{E}_{f}(s)ds+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{\kappa}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)^{2}.

This implies, under the assumption
(\varepsilon\delta)^{-1}\||{\rm div}U\||_{\tau_{0},2}+\delta^{-1}\||\textbf{NS}(U)\||_{\tau_{0},2}\lesssim\varepsilon^{0.99}
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}\left\{1-(\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\zeta_{f}(t)^{2})^{2}-\delta\varepsilon\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{Y}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim O(1)\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}+\delta^{2}\kappa^{-1}\zeta_{f}(t)^{4}+\varepsilon^{2}\kappa^{-1}\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}^{2}+\varepsilon^{0.99}.

Under the assumption that

\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\zeta_{f}(t)+\varepsilon\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\ll\delta,

(4.1)

we will have

\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)+\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\mathcal{Y}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\delta.

(4.2)

It suffices to check the compatibility of the above conditions, and the result follows by bootstrap argument.
As for
L^{\infty}
analytic norm
\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}
, we recall Theorem
3.27

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{H}_{h}|_{t=0}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\kappa^{-1}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{0.99}.

It suffices to have estimate on
\zeta_{f}(t)
. From Theorem
3.32
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{p}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{0}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

(4.3)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}+\varepsilon^{0.99}+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\zeta_{f}(t)^{2}.

At the same time, from Theorem
3.30
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\kappa^{\frac{1}{6}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{3}}+\left\|\mathcal{Y}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{Y}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\right\}\cdot\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{0.99}+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\right\}+\delta\varepsilon\left\{\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\right\}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{\rho,f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\zeta_{f}(t)\left\{\zeta_{f}(t)+\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{P}\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\right\}.

Combining the above with
(
4.3
)
, the assumptions
(
4.2
)
and
(
4.1
)
, we have

\displaystyle\zeta_{f}(t)\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{0}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{0.99}+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\zeta_{f}(t)^{2}+\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{Y}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\cdot\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\delta+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\zeta_{f}(t)^{2}+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\zeta_{f}(t)\zeta_{\partial_{t}f}(t).

The estimate
(
4.1
)
for
\zeta_{f}(t)
is satisfied, given

\displaystyle\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{0}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\delta\kappa^{-1}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{Y}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\kappa^{-1}\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\zeta_{\partial_{t}f}(t)

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma_{+}})\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{\gamma_{+}}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\delta^{2}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim 1.

We can take
\varepsilon=\kappa^{2M}
and
\delta=\kappa^{M}
for
M
large. The fluid solution is selected so that

\left\|{\rm div}U\right\|_{\tau_{0},2}+\left\|\textbf{NS}(U)\right\|_{\tau_{0},2}\lesssim\kappa^{100M}.

We see that we need control on the analytic norm of
\partial_{t}f
. The idea is that the equation for
\partial_{t}f
now is a linear equation, given the analytic bound of
f
.
We have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\kappa\varepsilon^{2}}L\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}+\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}-\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{(\partial_{t}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\xi\cdot\nabla_{x})\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\right\}f\right\}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\kappa{\varepsilon}^{2}}[\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha},L]f-\frac{1}{\varepsilon\delta}\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\textbf{R}_{a}\right\}+\frac{2\delta}{\kappa\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(\partial_{t}f,f)+\frac{2}{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},\partial_{t}f)+\frac{2}{\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma({\bf{f_{2}}},\partial_{t}f).

Using Theorem
3.15
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{2}+O(1)\left\|\mathcal{D}_{\partial_{t}f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}_{\gamma})\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}_{\gamma}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim o(1)\mathcal{E}_{f}(t)\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t,x,\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}+\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+o(1)\left\|\mathcal{D}_{\partial_{t}f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}+\left\|\mathcal{E}_{\partial_{t}f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\delta\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|\mathcal{D}_{\partial_{t}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\mathcal{E}_{\partial_{t}f}\right)\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}\partial_{t}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\left\|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

The proof is complete, since there is no loss of derivative coming from the nonlinear term of the right hand side. This gives us full control on
\left\|\mathcal{D}_{\partial_{t}f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}
. Finally, we note that

\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{D}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\left\|\mathcal{D}_{\partial_{t}f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}+\varepsilon^{0.99}\left\|\mathcal{E}_{f}\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}}.

The proof is complete.

5
Appendix

5.1
Elementary lemmas

Lemma 5.1

(
[
54
]
)
.

Let
R>0
and
n\geq 1,n\in\mathbb{N}
. There holds

\sum_{k_{1}+2k_{2}+\cdots+nk_{n}=n}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{n})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{n}!}R^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{n}}=R(R+1)^{n-1}.

(5.1)

Proof.

We first show
(
5.1
)
. We define

g(x)=\frac{1}{1-x},\qquad f(x)=\frac{1}{1-R(x-1)},\qquad h(x)=f(g(x))=\frac{1-x}{1-(R+1)x}.

It is clear that

\displaystyle g(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}x^{n}=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{g^{(n)}(0)}{n!}x^{n}

(5.2)

\displaystyle f(x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}R^{n}x^{n}=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{f^{(n)}(0)}{n!}x^{n}

\displaystyle h(x)

\displaystyle=1+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}R(R+1)^{n-1}x^{n}.

At the same time, by the chain rule, we have

h^{(n)}(x)=\sum_{k_{1}+2k_{2}+\cdots+nk_{n}=n}\frac{n!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{n}!}f^{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{n})}(g(x))\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{g^{(i)}(x)}{i!}\right)^{k_{i}}.

Putting
x=0
in the above equation and using
(
5.2
)
, we have, for
n\geq 1
:

R(R+1)^{n-1}=\sum_{k_{1}+2k_{2}+\cdots+nk_{n}=n}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{n})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{n}!}R^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{n}}.

This proves
(
5.1
)
.
∎

Lemma 5.2
.

Let
d\in\mathbb{N},d\geq 2
and
R>0
. There exists a constant
C_{d}>0
only depending on
d
such that

\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
|\alpha|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}R^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}}\leq C_{d}\min\{R,1\}e^{2^{d}R}.

(5.3)

Proof.

We define the functions
g:\{x\in\mathbb{\mathbb{R}}^{d}:\quad\|x\|<1\}\to\mathbb{R}
and
f:\mathbb{R}-\{1\}\to\mathbb{R}
such that

g(x)=\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}}x^{\alpha}=\prod_{i=1}^{d}\frac{1}{1-x_{i}},\qquad f(t)=\frac{1}{1-R(t-1)}=(1+R-Rt)^{-1},

where

x^{\alpha}=x_{1}^{\alpha_{1}}x_{2}^{\alpha_{2}}\cdots x_{d}^{\alpha_{d}},\qquad x\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\quad\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}.

For
n\geq 1
, we have

f^{(n)}(t)=n!R^{n}(1+R-Rt)^{-(n+1)}.

Define

h(x)=f(g(x))=\frac{1}{1-R(g(x)-1)}

Now using the Faà di Bruno’s formula, we have

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}h(x)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}R^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}}(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s})!

\displaystyle\times(1+R-Rg(x))^{-(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}+1)}\left\{\frac{1}{\beta_{1}!}\partial^{\beta_{1}}g\right\}^{k_{1}}\left\{\frac{1}{\beta_{1}!}\partial^{\beta_{2}}g\right\}^{k_{2}}\cdots\left\{\frac{1}{\beta_{s}!}\partial^{\beta_{1}}g\right\}^{k_{s}}.

Now putting
x=0
in the above equation and using the fact that
\partial^{\alpha}g|_{x=0}=\alpha!
, we get

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}R^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}}=\frac{1}{\alpha!}\partial^{\alpha}h|_{x=0}.

(5.4)

Now we compute the left right side of the above, by expanding
h
around
x=0
. Denote

\vec{c}_{0}=(c_{0},c_{0},\cdots,c_{0})\in\mathbb{R}^{d}.

We have

h(\vec{c}_{0})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}\\
|\alpha|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\partial^{\alpha}h|_{x=0}\frac{c_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}

(5.5)

Since
g(\vec{c}_{0})=(1-c_{0})^{-d}
, we have

h(\vec{c}_{0})=f(g(\vec{c}_{0}))=f((1-c_{0})^{-d})=\frac{1}{1-R((1-c_{0})^{-d}-1)}=\widetilde{f}(\widetilde{g}(c_{0})),

(5.6)

where

\widetilde{f}(t)=\frac{1}{1-Rt},\qquad\widetilde{g}(t)=(1-t)^{-d}-1.

Now we have

\widetilde{f}(\widetilde{g}(c_{0}))=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{n!}(f(\widetilde{g}(c_{0})))^{(n)}|_{c_{0}=0}c_{0}^{n}.

By the Faà di Bruno’s formula, we have

(\widetilde{f}(\widetilde{g}(c_{0}))^{(n)}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n}=k\\
b_{1}+2b_{2}+\cdots+nb_{n}=n\end{subarray}}\widetilde{f}^{(k)}(\widetilde{g}(c_{0}))\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{\widetilde{g}^{(i)}(c_{0})}{i!}\right\}^{b_{i}}.

Plugging
c_{0}=0
in the above and using the fact that
\widetilde{f}^{(n)}(0)=R^{n},\widetilde{g}^{(n)}(0)=d(d+1)\cdots(d+n-1)
, we have

(\widetilde{f}(\widetilde{g}(c_{0}))^{(n)}|_{c_{0}=0}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n}=k\\
b_{1}+2b_{2}+\cdots+nb_{n}=n\end{subarray}}R^{k}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{d(d+1)\cdots(d+i-1)}{i!}\right\}^{b_{i}}.

Hence

h(\vec{c}_{0})=\widetilde{f}(\widetilde{g}(c_{0}))=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{1}{n!}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n}=k\\
b_{1}+2b_{2}+\cdots+nb_{n}=n\end{subarray}}R^{k}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{d(d+1)\cdots(d+i-1)}{i!}\right\}^{b_{i}}\right)c_{0}^{n}.

Combining the above with
(
5.5
)
, we have

\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\partial^{\alpha}h|_{x=0}}{\alpha!}x^{|\alpha|}=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{1}{n!}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n}=k\\
b_{1}+2b_{2}+\cdots+nb_{n}=n\end{subarray}}R^{k}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{d(d+1)\cdots(d+i-1)}{i!}\right\}^{b_{i}}\right)x^{n}.

Equation
x^{n}
on both sides, we have

\sum_{|\alpha|=n}\frac{\partial^{\alpha}h|_{x=0}}{\alpha!}=\frac{1}{n!}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n}=k\\
b_{1}+2b_{2}+\cdots+nb_{n}=n\end{subarray}}R^{k}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{d(d+1)\cdots(d+i-1)}{i!}\right\}^{b_{i}}.

Now combining the above with
(
5.4
)
, we have, for
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}
with
|\alpha|=n
:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}R^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}}

(5.7)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n!}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n}=k\\
b_{1}+2b_{2}+\cdots+nb_{n}=n\end{subarray}}R^{k}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{d(d+1)\cdots(d+i-1)}{i!}\right\}^{b_{i}}.

Now we note that

\frac{d(d+1)\cdots(d+i-1)}{i!}=\binom{d+i-1}{i}\leq\sum_{i=0}^{d}\binom{d+i-1}{i}=2^{d+i-1}.

Hence

\prod_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{d(d+1)\cdots(d+i-1)}{i!}\right\}^{b_{i}}\leq 2^{dk+n-k}=2^{k(d-1)}2^{n}.

Combining the above with
(
5.7
)
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}R^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{n}}{n!}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1}+2b_{2}+\cdots+nb_{n}=n\end{subarray}}\left(2^{d-1}R\right)^{b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{n}}{n!}\sum_{b_{1}+2b_{2}+\cdots+nb_{n}=n}\frac{(b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n})!}{b_{1}!b_{2}!\cdots b_{n}!}(2^{d-1}R)^{b_{1}+b_{2}+\cdots+b_{n}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{n}}{n!}2^{d-1}R(1+2^{d-1}R)^{n-1}\lesssim\min\{R,1\}\frac{2^{n}}{n!}(1+2^{d-1}R)^{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\{R,1\}\frac{1}{n!}(2+2^{d}R)^{n}\lesssim\min\{R,1\}e^{2^{d}R},

where we used
(
5.1
)
in the above. The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma 5.3
.

[Analyticity estimate for composite functions]. Let
H:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
and
G:\mathbb{\mathbb{R}}\to\mathbb{R}
satisfy

\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}\\
|\alpha|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\tau_{H}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}H\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim R,\qquad\sup_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau_{G}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}G\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim 1.

(5.8)

There holds

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{d}\\
|\alpha|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\tau_{H}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(G(H(x))\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim\min\{R,1\}e^{\tau_{H}+2^{d}\tau_{G}^{-1}R}.

Proof.

By the Faà di Bruno’s formula, we have

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}\left(G(H(x))\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}\partial_{X}^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}}G|_{X=H(x)}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\frac{1}{\beta_{1}!}\partial^{\beta_{1}}H\right\}^{k_{1}}\left\{\frac{1}{\beta_{1}!}\partial^{\beta_{2}}H\right\}^{k_{2}}\cdots\left\{\frac{1}{\beta_{s}!}\partial^{\beta_{1}}H\right\}^{k_{s}}.

Combining the above with
(
5.8
)
, we have

\frac{\tau_{H}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}|\partial^{\alpha}G(H(x))|\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}\left(\frac{R}{\tau_{G}}\right)^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}}.

Now using Lemma
5.2
for the right hand side in the above, we get

\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}\\
|\alpha|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\tau_{H}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}|\partial^{\alpha}\left\{G(H(x)\right\}|\lesssim\min\{R,1\}e^{2^{d}\tau_{G}^{-1}R}.

The proof is complete, by summing over
\alpha
.
∎

Lemma 5.4
.

Let
A>0
,
R_{0}\in(0,1)
. There holds

\sup_{n\geq 0}\frac{R_{0}^{n}}{n!}\left|\partial_{s}^{n}(e^{-As^{2}})\right|\leq e^{-\frac{A}{2}s^{2}},

and

\sup_{n\geq 0}\frac{R_{0}^{n}}{n!}|\partial_{s}^{n}(e^{As^{2}})|\lesssim e^{3As^{2}}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle|\partial_{s}^{n}(e^{-As^{2}})|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b_{1}+2b_{2}=n\\
b_{1}+b_{2}=k\end{subarray}}e^{-As^{2}}\frac{n!}{b_{1}!b_{2}!}(-2As)^{b_{1}}(-A)^{b_{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-As^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{n!}{k!}\sum_{b_{1}+b_{2}=k}\frac{k!}{b_{1}!b_{2}!}(2A|s|+A)^{b_{1}+b_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-As^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{n!}{k!}(2A|s|+2A)^{k}.

Hence

\frac{R_{0}^{n}}{n!}|\partial_{s}^{n}(e^{-As^{2}})|\lesssim R_{0}^{n}e^{-As^{2}+2A|s|}\lesssim e^{-\frac{A}{2}s^{2}}.

The proof is complete. The second inequality is similar.
∎

Lemma 5.5
.

Let
d\geq 3
and
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
such that

\sup_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{f}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}|\partial^{\alpha}f(x)|\lesssim A_{f}\qquad\text{for all}\quad x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}

There holds

\sup_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{f}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}|\partial^{\alpha}(e^{sf(x)})|\lesssim A_{f}\exp\left(sf(x)+2^{d-1}A_{f}|s|\right).

Proof.

By the Faà di Bruno’s formula, we have

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}

\displaystyle(e^{sf(x)})=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots,k_{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{\ell}\beta_{\ell}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{\ell}!}s^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{\ell}}e^{sf(x)}\prod_{i=1}^{\ell}\left\{\frac{1}{\beta_{i}!}\partial^{\beta_{i}}f\right\}^{k_{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha!e^{sf(x)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots,k_{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{\ell}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{\ell}\beta_{\ell}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{\ell})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{\ell}!}(|s|A_{f})^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{\ell}}\tau_{f}^{-|\alpha|}.

Now combining the above with Lemma
5.2
, we have

\sup_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{f}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}|\partial^{\alpha}(e^{sf(x)})|\lesssim e^{sf(x)}\min\{|s|A_{f},1\}e^{2^{d}|s|A_{f}}\lesssim e^{sf(x)}A_{f}e^{2^{d-1}A_{f}|s|}.

This completes the proof.
∎

5.2
Bilinear estimates

Lemma 5.6
.

There holds

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|f(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|g(\xi^{\prime})\right|\left|h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|f(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left(\left\|\sqrt{\nu}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|h\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}h\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|f(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|g(\xi^{\prime})\right|\left|h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|f(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|f(\xi_{\star})g(\xi)h(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|f(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\nu(\xi)^{-1/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|f(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|g(\xi^{\prime})\right|\left|h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|f(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\left\|\int_{|{\omega}|=1}\sqrt{\nu}(\xi)|g(\xi^{\prime})||h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})|d{\omega}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi_{\star}}}.

Hence

\displaystyle\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|f(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|g(\xi^{\prime})\right|\left|h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\{\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|f(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\}\left(\left\|\sqrt{\nu}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|h\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}h\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right).

Here we used the fact that
\nu(\xi)\lesssim\nu(\xi^{\prime})+\nu(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})
and

d\xi d\xi_{\star}=d\xi^{\prime}d\xi_{\star}^{\prime}
.
Next we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}||f(\xi_{\star})||g(\xi)||h(\xi_{\star})|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim|g(\xi)|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|f(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

The proof is complete.
∎

Lemma 5.7
.

Recall the definition of
\Gamma_{\pm}
in
(
3.2
)
. There holds

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma_{+}(f,g)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\sqrt{\nu}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma_{-}(f,g)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\sqrt{\nu}f\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(f,g)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\sqrt{\nu}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Proof.

Using Lemma
(
5.6
)
, we get

\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma_{+}(f,g)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\left\|\sqrt{\nu}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Now we apply Lemma
5.6
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\frac{f}{\sqrt{\nu}}(\xi)g(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|g\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\frac{f}{\sqrt{\nu}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\|\sqrt{\nu}f\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Proposition 5.8
.

Recall the definition of
\Gamma
in
(
3.2
)

There holds

\displaystyle\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,g),h\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left(\left\|\sqrt{\nu}\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}\partial^{\gamma}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})h\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\|h\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\sup_{\xi}\left\{\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left\||\xi-\xi_{\star}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi_{\star}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|\nu\partial^{\gamma}f\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\partial^{\varrho}g\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\|\partial^{\gamma}f\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nu\partial^{\varrho}g\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right).

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{+}(f,g)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left(\partial^{\gamma}f(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}g(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+\partial^{\gamma}g(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{-}(f,g)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left(\partial^{\gamma}f(\xi)\partial^{\varrho}g(\xi_{\star})+\partial^{\gamma}g(\xi)\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}.

This implies

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,g)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\Gamma(\partial^{\beta}f,\partial^{\gamma}g)
