\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left(\partial^{\gamma}f(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\varrho}g(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+\partial^{\gamma}g(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left(\partial^{\gamma}f(\xi)\partial^{\varrho}g(\xi_{\star})+\partial^{\gamma}g(\xi)\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}.

For the first term, we have

\langle\Gamma(\partial^{\beta}f,\partial^{\gamma}g),h\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}=\langle\Gamma(\partial^{\beta}f,\partial^{\gamma}g),({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})h\rangle_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(\partial^{\beta}f,\partial^{\gamma}g)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})h\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Now using Lemma
5.7
, we have

\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\Gamma(\partial^{\beta}f,\partial^{\gamma}g)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\left\|\sqrt{\nu}\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}\partial^{\gamma}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Next we bound

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left(\partial^{\gamma}f(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}g(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+\partial^{\gamma}g(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}.

Using similar ideas as in Lemma
5.6
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left(\partial^{\gamma}f(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}g(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+\partial^{\gamma}g(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left(\|\nu\partial^{\gamma}f\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\partial^{\rho}g\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\|\partial^{\gamma}f\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nu\partial^{\rho}g\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right).

The proof is complete.
∎

Proposition 5.9
.

Let
\beta,\gamma,\rho\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
and
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
such that
\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha
and
\varrho<\alpha
. There holds, for a sequence of functions
\{T_{\varrho}\}_{\varrho\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\right|\left|T_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\cdot 1_{\{|\gamma|\geq 1,\gamma+\rho=\alpha\}}\cdot\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nu^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\cdot 1_{\{|\beta|\geq 1\}}\cdot\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\cdot\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Proof.

Using Lemma
5.6
and then consider
\beta\neq 0
or
\beta=0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\right|\left|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\cdot 1_{\{|\beta|\geq 1\}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\cdot 1_{\{|\gamma|\geq 1\}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Here, for
|\beta|=0
, we use lemma
2.3
to get

\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\lesssim\nu_{0}(\varphi)=\nu(\xi).

The second inequality is shown similarly. This completes the proof. ∎

Proposition 5.10
.

Let
\beta,\gamma,\varrho\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
and
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
such that
\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha
and
\varrho<\alpha
. There holds, for a sequence of functions
\{T_{\varrho}\}_{\varrho\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime}_{\star})\right|\left|T_{\varrho}(\xi^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1_{\{\gamma+\rho=\alpha,|\gamma|\geq 1\}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+1_{\{|\beta|\geq 1\}}\left\|\nu^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Proof.

Applying Lemma
5.6
and then consider
\beta\neq 0
or
\beta=0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime}_{\star})\right|\left|T_{\varrho}(\xi^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1_{\{\gamma+\rho=\alpha,|\gamma|\geq 1\}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+1_{\{|\beta|\geq 1\}}\left\|\nu^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\sqrt{\nu}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Here, we used Lemma
2.3
for the case
|\beta|=0
. This completes the proof.
∎

Proposition 5.11
.

Let
\beta,\gamma,\varrho,\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
such that
\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha
and
\rho<\alpha
. There holds, for any sequence
\{T_{\varrho}\}_{\varrho\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\right|\left|T_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}\nu(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

and

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})T_{\gamma}(\xi)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{\gamma}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Proof.

Applying Lemma
5.6
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)T_{\varrho}(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\sqrt{\nu}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}\nu(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\nu^{-1/2}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\sqrt{\nu}T_{\varrho}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}\nu(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

As for the second inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\cdot T_{\gamma}(\xi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{\gamma}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

The proof is complete.∎

Lemma 5.12
.

There holds

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|f(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|g(\xi^{\prime})\right|\left|h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\langle\xi\rangle f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\min\{\left\|g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},\left\|f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},\}

and

\left\|\nu^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|f(\xi_{\star})g(\xi)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\left\|g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+\left\|\nu^{-1}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle f\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\left|f(\xi_{\star})\right|\left|g(\xi^{\prime})\right|\left|h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right|d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|f(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|g(\xi^{\prime})|^{2}|h(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})|^{2}d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\langle\xi\rangle f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\min\{\left\|g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},\left\|f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}.

Next, we have

\displaystyle\nu(\xi)^{-1}g(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu(\xi)^{-1}|g(\xi)|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(|\xi|+|\xi_{\star}|)|f(\xi_{\star})|d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle\lesssim|g(\xi)|\left\|f\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+\nu^{-1}(\xi)g(\xi)\left\|\langle\xi\rangle f\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Proposition 5.13
.

There holds, for any
p_{0}\ll 1

\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,g)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\lesssim\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|e^{p_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}\cdot\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\left\|e^{p_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\right\|_{\ell_{\alpha}^{2}}.

Proof.

We recall from
(
3.1
)
and then use Lemma
5.12
to get

\displaystyle\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{+}(f,g)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\min\left\{\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\varrho}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}},\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\varrho}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\varrho}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{-}(f,g)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}(\sqrt{\mu}g)\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+\left\|\nu^{-1}\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle\partial^{\gamma}(\sqrt{\mu}g)\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\beta}(\sqrt{\mu}g)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}+\left\|\nu^{-1}\partial^{\beta}(\sqrt{\mu}g)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\langle\xi\rangle\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{1}_{\xi}}\right\}

The proof is complete, by using Proposition
2.3
and the discrete Young inequality.
∎

Proposition 5.14
.

Recalling
(
3.7
)
,
There holds

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\langle\partial^{\alpha}(V(x)({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f(x,\xi)),\partial^{\alpha}g\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\||V\||_{\tau_{0},\infty}\mathcal{E}_{f}\left(\mathcal{E}_{g}+\mathcal{D}_{g}\right).

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\langle\partial^{\alpha}(V(x)({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f),\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}=\sum_{\beta+\gamma=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\partial^{\beta}V\partial^{\gamma}\left\{({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f\right\},\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\langle\partial^{\alpha}V({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P}),\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\partial^{\beta}V\partial^{\gamma}\left\{({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f\right\},\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

Now for
|\gamma|\geq 1
, we have

\partial^{\gamma}(({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f)=\partial^{\gamma}f-\partial^{\gamma}\mathbf{P}f=\partial^{\gamma}f-\mathbf{P}\partial^{\gamma}f+[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\gamma}]f=({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\gamma}f+[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\gamma}]f.

This implies

\displaystyle\langle\partial^{\alpha}(V(x)({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f),\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle=\langle\partial^{\alpha}V({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f,({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\partial^{\beta}V({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\gamma}f,({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\langle\partial^{\beta}V[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\gamma}]f,\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

Hence we get

\displaystyle\langle\partial^{\alpha}(V(x)({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f),\partial^{\alpha}g\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial^{\alpha}V\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\partial^{\beta}V\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbf{I}}-\mathbf{P})\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\partial^{\beta}V\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|[\mathbf{P},\partial^{\gamma}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}.

The proof is complete, by applying Lemma
5.18
and the discrete Young inequality.
∎

5.3
Commutator estimates

Lemma 5.15
.

Assume

(
3.8
)
.
Then for all
m>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

(5.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}(a,b,c)\right\|_{L^{2}}.

As for derivatives, there hold

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial_{x_{3}}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

(5.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left(\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}(a,b,c)\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}(a,b,c)\right\|_{L^{2}}.

For
i\in\{1,2\}
, there holds

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial_{x_{i}}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

(5.11)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left(\varepsilon\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{i}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}(a,b,c)\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{i}}(a,b,c)\right\|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

Recalling
a_{\alpha},b_{\alpha},c_{\alpha}
defined in
(
3.9
)
. We have

\displaystyle[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f=\partial^{\alpha}(\mathbf{P}f)-\mathbf{P}(\partial^{\alpha}f)

\displaystyle=\left\{\partial^{\alpha}(a\sqrt{\mu})-a_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu}\right\}+\left\{\partial^{\alpha}(b\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu})-b_{\alpha}\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu}\right\}+\left\{\partial^{\alpha}\left(c\frac{|\varphi|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}\right)-c_{\alpha}\frac{|\varphi|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}\right\}

\displaystyle=I_{a}+I_{b}+I_{c}.

where we denote, in this proof,

\begin{cases}I_{a}&=\partial^{\alpha}(a\sqrt{\mu})-a_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu}\\
I_{b}&=\partial^{\alpha}(b\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu})-b_{\alpha}\cdot\varphi\sqrt{\mu}\\
I_{c}&=\partial^{\alpha}(c\frac{|\varphi|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu})-c_{\alpha}\frac{|\varphi|^{2}-3}{2}\sqrt{\mu}\end{cases}.

We will treat
I_{a}
only, as the proof for
I_{b},I_{c}
is similar. We have

\displaystyle I_{a}

\displaystyle=\partial^{\alpha}(a\sqrt{\mu})-a_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\gamma}a\cdot\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}-a_{\alpha}\sqrt{\mu}

(5.12)

\displaystyle=(\partial^{\alpha}a-a_{\alpha})\sqrt{\mu}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\gamma}a\cdot\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}.

Now

\partial^{\alpha}a-a_{\alpha}=\partial^{\alpha}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f\sqrt{\mu}d\xi\right)-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{\alpha}f\sqrt{\mu}d\xi=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\cdot\partial^{\gamma}fd\xi.

(5.13)

Combining
(
5.12
)
and
(
5.13
)
, we have

I_{a}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\sqrt{\mu}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\cdot\partial^{\gamma}fd\xi+\partial^{\gamma}a\cdot\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\}.

(5.14)

This implies

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}I_{a}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left|\partial^{\gamma}a\right|\right\},

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}I_{a}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}a\right\|_{L^{2}}\right\}

The proof of
(
5.9
)
is complete. Now we show
(
5.10
)
. From
(
5.14
)
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{x_{3}}I_{a}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\cdot\partial^{\gamma}fd\xi+\partial^{\gamma}a\cdot\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\sqrt{\mu}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\cdot\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}fd\xi+\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}a\cdot\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\sqrt{\mu}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\cdot\partial^{\gamma}fd\xi.

This implies

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial_{x_{3}}I_{a}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left|\partial^{\gamma}a\right|\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}a\right|\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Taking
L^{2}
in
x\in\Omega
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial_{x_{3}}I_{a}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left(\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\gamma}a\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}a\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}.

Here we use Proposition
2.3
in the last estimate. The proof of
(
5.10
)
is complete. The proof of
(
5.11
)
is similar, and we skip the details.
∎

Corollary 5.16
.

If
\mathbf{P}f=0
, there holds

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{3}}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left(\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

(5.15)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\beta}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{i}}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left(\varepsilon\left\|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{i}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

(5.16)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\beta}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{i}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

There also hold the analytic estimates

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{3}}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

(5.17)

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{i}}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

(5.18)

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{i}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Proof.

The proof follows directly from the previous proposition, using the fact that
(a,b,c)\equiv 0
and skipping the
x
integration. The inequality
(
5.17
)
follows from Proposition
2.3
and Lemma
(
2.4
)
. Similarly,
(
5.18
)
follows from
(
5.16
)
and
(
2.4
)
.
∎

Proposition 5.17
.

Assume
(
3.8
)
. For
\tau\leq\tau_{0}
small defined in Proposition
2.3
, we have

\|\partial\mathbf{P}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\partial f\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\sqrt{\kappa}\|f\|_{L^{2}}\qquad\forall\partial\in\{\varepsilon\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{1}},\partial_{x_{2}}\}.

(5.19)

Moreover, there holds the analytic estimate

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

(5.20)

and

\left(\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\lesssim\left(\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

(5.21)

Proof.

We show the bound for
a
only, as the other cases are similar. We have

a(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f(t,x,\xi)\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)d\xi

Take
\partial\in\{\varepsilon\partial_{t},\partial_{x_{1}},\partial_{x_{2}}\}
and
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}a

\displaystyle=\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\binom{\alpha}{\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{\beta}f\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\sqrt{\mu}d\xi\lesssim\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!(\alpha-\beta)!}\left\|\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

(5.22)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!(\alpha-\beta)!}\left\|\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\left\|\partial^{\alpha}a\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!(\alpha-\beta)!}\left\|\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Using Proposition
2.3
, we get
\left\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim 1_{\{|\alpha-\beta|\geq 1\}}\varepsilon\sqrt{\kappa}+1_{\{\beta=\alpha\}}
. Combining this with
(
5.22
)
, we get

\left\|\partial^{\alpha}a\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\sqrt{\kappa}\sum_{\beta<\alpha}\|\partial^{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}}.

This shows the inequality
(
5.21
)
. As for the second inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}a\right\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\sum_{\beta\leq\alpha}\frac{\tau^{|\beta|}}{\beta!}\|\partial^{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\frac{\tau^{|\alpha-\beta|}}{|\alpha-\beta|!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\cdot\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!(\alpha-\beta)!}\cdot\frac{\beta!|\alpha-\beta|!}{\alpha!}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}},

again, by using Proposition
2.3
. This finishes the proof of
(
5.20
)
. The second inequality is done similarly. The proof is complete.
∎

Proposition 5.18
.

For all
m>0
, there hold the following estimates

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}},

(5.23)

and

\left\{\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\left\{\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{2}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}^{2}\right\}^{1/2}.

(5.24)

Proof.

Using Lemma
5.19
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}[\partial^{\alpha},\mathbf{P}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\tau^{|\beta|}}{\beta!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}L^{\infty}_{x}}\frac{\tau^{|\gamma|}}{\gamma!}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\tau^{|\beta|}}{\beta!}\left\|\langle\varphi\rangle^{m}\partial^{\beta}\left\{(1,\varphi,|\varphi|^{2})\sqrt{\mu}\right\}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\xi}}\frac{\tau^{|\gamma|}}{\gamma!}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}(a,b,c)\right\|_{L^{2}}.

The result follows directly from Proposition
2.3
and Proposition
5.20
.
∎

Proposition 5.19
.

There hold

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

(5.25)

and

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{-1}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left(\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right).

(5.26)

There also hold

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}[L,\partial_{x_{3}}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}[L,\partial_{x_{i}}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{i}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\varepsilon\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},\qquad 1\leq i\leq 2

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1}[L,\partial_{x_{3}}]f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\nu^{-1}[L,\partial_{x_{i}}]f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|\partial_{x_{i}}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|\partial_{x_{i}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right)+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\left\|f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right),\quad 1\leq i\leq 2.

Proof.

We show the first inequality, and the others are similar. As in
(
3.47
)
, we have

\displaystyle\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f=L(\partial^{\alpha}f)-\partial^{\alpha}Lf

\displaystyle=\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}f(\xi)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle=A_{\alpha}+B_{\alpha}+C_{\alpha}+D_{\alpha}.

Using Propositions
5.9
,
5.10
,
5.11
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|A_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\rho!\gamma!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|B_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\rho!\gamma!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|C_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|+|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}\nu(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|D_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

The proof of
(
5.25
)
is complete, using Lemma
2.3
. The proof of
(
5.26
)
is done similarly, by using Lemma
5.12
and Lemma
2.3
. We skip the details.
∎

Proposition 5.20
.

There holds

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{3}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

(5.27)

For
i\in\{1,2\}
, there hold

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{i}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{i}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\varepsilon\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

(5.28)

Proof.

We show
(
5.27
)
only, as the proof of
(
5.28
)
similar.
Using
(
3.47
)
, we have

\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{3}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]f=\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}](\partial_{x_{3}}f)+A_{1,\alpha}+A_{2,\alpha}+B_{1,\alpha}+B_{2,\alpha}-C_{1,\alpha}-C_{2,\alpha}-D_{\alpha},

where

\displaystyle A_{\alpha,1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle A_{\alpha,2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle B_{\alpha,1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle B_{\alpha,2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\partial^{\rho}f(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle C_{1,\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle C_{\alpha,2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
\rho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\partial^{\rho}f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star},

\displaystyle D_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\gamma<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|{\omega}|=1}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\mu(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}f(\xi)d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}.

Using Proposition
5.9
,
5.10
,
5.11
, we obtain the following estimates

\displaystyle\left\|A_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\rho!\gamma!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|A_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Using Proposition
5.10
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|B_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\rho!\gamma!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|B_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\rho!\gamma!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x_{3}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\xi_{\star}}}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Using Proposition
5.11
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|C_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|+|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}\nu(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\left\|C_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|+|\gamma|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\rho!}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\left(\int_{\xi_{\star}}\nu(\xi_{\star})^{-1}|\xi-\xi_{\star}|^{2}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})|^{2}d\xi_{\star}\right)^{1/2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\partial^{\gamma}\partial_{x_{3}}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\rho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Hence we get

\left\|D_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\rho=\alpha\\
|\beta|\geq 1\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\gamma}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Using Propositions
2.3
and
(
2.4
)
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\{\left\|A_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|A_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|B_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|B_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|C_{\alpha,1}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|C_{\alpha,2}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\left\|D_{\alpha}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Lastly, applying Theorem
5.19
, we get

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}[L,\partial^{\alpha}]\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{3}}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

5.4
Analytic estimates on
L^{-1}
and bounds on
A_{ij}(\varphi)

Lemma 5.21
.

Recall the definition of
\nu
in
(
3.17
)
.
There exists
\tau_{0}>0
such that

\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}(\nu^{-1}(\xi))\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle|\partial^{\alpha}(\nu(\xi)^{-1})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s})!\prod_{i=1}^{s}\left\{\frac{1}{\beta_{i}!}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\beta_{i}}\nu\right\}^{k_{i}}.

Using Lemma
2.3
, we have

\sup_{|\beta_{i}|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\beta_{i}|}}{\beta_{i}!}\nu^{-1}(\xi)\partial^{\beta_{i}}\nu\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Hence

\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}|\partial^{\alpha}(\nu(\xi)^{-1})|\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},k_{2},\cdots k_{s}\in\mathbb{N}\\
\beta_{1},\beta_{2},\cdots,\beta_{s}\in\mathbb{N}^{d}\\
k_{1}\beta_{1}+k_{2}\beta_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}\beta_{s}=\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{(k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s})!}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{s}!}(\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k_{1}+k_{2}+\cdots+k_{s}}.

Combining the above with Lemma
5.1
, we get, for any
c_{0}\in(0,1)
:

\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{(c_{0}\tau_{0})^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}|\partial^{\alpha}(\nu(\xi)^{-1})|\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1-c_{0})^{d}-\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

The proof is complete.
∎

Proposition 5.22
.

For
g\in\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
, there holds, for
\rho\ll 1

\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}L^{-1}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

(5.29)

Proof.

We have, for
\rho\in(0,\frac{1}{4})
:

\left\|\nu_{0}(\xi-\varepsilon U)e^{\rho|\xi-\varepsilon U|^{2}}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\left\|e^{\rho|\varphi|^{2}}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|\nu_{0}(\varphi)^{-1}e^{\rho|\varphi|^{2}}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\varphi}}.

Hence

\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|\nu(\xi)^{-1}e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

for
\rho\ll 1
.
∎

Proposition 5.23
.

Assume that
Lf=g
. There holds, for
\rho\ll 1
, the estimate

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Let
\ell\in\{1,2,3\}
. There holds

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{\ell}}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\{\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{\ell}}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\right\},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{t}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\{\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{t}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\right\}.

Proof.

We recall that

\displaystyle-Lf

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left\{\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+f(\xi^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right\}d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

(5.30)

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-f(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

Take
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
and apply
\partial^{\alpha}
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle-L(\partial^{\alpha}f)

(5.31)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left\{\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right\}d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\beta<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\partial^{\beta}f(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle=\partial^{\alpha}g.

By the
L^{2}
estimate
(
3.20
)
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime}_{\star})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\beta<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\beta}f(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Applying Lemma
5.6
and the fact that
|\xi|^{2}\leq|\xi_{\star}^{\prime}|^{2}+|\xi^{\prime}|^{2}
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime}_{\star})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\varrho}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|e^{2\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\left\|e^{2\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}.

Similarly, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\beta}f(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\beta}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\left\|e^{2\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}.

Combining all of the above inequalities with Lemma
2.3
, we get

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

The proof is complete.
Next we show the analytic
L^{\infty}
estimate. Again from
(
5.31
)

and the inequality
(
5.29
)
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime}_{\star})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha\\
\varrho<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\beta}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\partial^{\varrho}f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta+\gamma=\alpha\\
\beta<\alpha\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\beta}f(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}.

Similarly as the above
L^{2}
estimate above, we
use Lemma
5.12
and Lemma
2.3
to get

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\nu(\xi)^{-1}\partial^{\alpha}g\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\{\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}}\frac{\tau^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}f\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\right\}.

The proof is complete.
Now we show the third inequality in Proposition
5.23
. From
(
5.30
)
, we have

\displaystyle-L(\partial_{x_{\ell}}f)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})\left\{\partial_{x_{\ell}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi^{\prime})f(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})+f(\xi^{\prime})\partial_{x_{\ell}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star}^{\prime})\right\}d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-\partial_{x_{\ell}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}-\sqrt{\mu}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial_{x_{\ell}}\sqrt{\mu}(\xi_{\star})f(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}

\displaystyle-f(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|(\xi-\xi_{\star})\cdot{\omega}|\partial_{x_{\ell}}\mu(\xi_{\star})d{\omega}d\xi_{\star}=\partial_{x_{\ell}}g.

The proof proceeds exactly as above, by using Lemma
2.4
and the fact that
\varepsilon\kappa^{-\frac{1}{2}}\ll 1
. We skip the details.
∎

Corollary 5.24
.

There exists universal constants
\tau_{0},\rho_{0}>0
such that

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}^{3}}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}(Id,\partial_{x_{\ell}},\partial_{t})A_{ij}(\varphi)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

Applying Proposition
5.23
and using the fact that
LA_{ij}=\hat{A}_{ij}=\left(\varphi_{i}\varphi_{j}-\frac{1}{3}\delta_{ij}|\varphi|^{2}\right)\sqrt{\mu}
, we need to check that

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}^{3}}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}\hat{A}_{ij}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 1

and

\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}^{3}}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|e^{\rho_{0}|\xi|^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x_{\ell}}\hat{A}_{ij}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\lesssim 1.

The above inequality follows from
(
5.4
)
.
∎

Proposition 5.25
.

Recall
\tau_{0}>0
to be the analyticity radius of
U
, and the definition of
\mu_{M}
in
(
3.14
)
. Let
T_{M}\in(0,1)
so that
|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\ll 1
.
There holds, for
\tau_{0}\ll 1
:

\sum_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}_{M}}A_{ij}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

We recall from
(
3.57
)
that

\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{\sqrt{\mu}_{M}}=T_{M}^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{4}}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}|U|^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U\cdot\xi}=T_{M}^{\frac{3}{2}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}e^{|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\frac{\xi_{\ell}^{2}}{4}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}U_{\ell}^{2}}.

Since the function
e^{-\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{2}U_{\ell}^{2}}
is analytic, it suffices to bound

\sup_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left(e^{|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\frac{\xi_{\ell}^{2}}{4}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}}A_{ij}(\varphi)\right)\right\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Using Lemma
5.5
and
5.4
and we have

\displaystyle\sup_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left|\partial^{\alpha}(e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon\xi_{\ell}U_{\ell}+4\varepsilon\kappa^{\frac{1}{2}}|\xi_{\ell}|}\lesssim e^{C_{0}\varepsilon(1+\|U\|_{L^{\infty}})|\xi|},

(5.32)

\displaystyle\sup_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\left|\partial^{\alpha}(e^{|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\frac{\xi_{\ell}^{2}}{4}})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{\frac{3|T_{M}^{-1}-1|}{4}\xi_{\ell}^{2}}\lesssim e^{\frac{3|T_{M}^{-1}-1|}{4}|\xi|^{2}}.

Now we have

\partial^{\alpha}\left(e^{|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\frac{\xi_{\ell}^{2}}{4}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}}A_{ij}(\varphi)\right)=\sum_{\beta+\gamma+\varrho=\alpha}\frac{\alpha!}{\beta!\gamma!\varrho!}\partial^{\beta}\left(e^{|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\frac{\xi_{\ell}^{2}}{4}}\right)\partial^{\gamma}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}}\right)\partial^{\varrho}\left(A_{ij}(\varphi)\right).

Combining the above with
(
5.32
)
and Corollary
5.24
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\alpha|}}{\alpha!}\partial^{\alpha}\left(e^{|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\frac{\xi_{\ell}^{2}}{4}+\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon U_{\ell}\xi_{\ell}}A_{ij}(\varphi)\right)\lesssim e^{\frac{3|T_{M}^{-1}-1|}{4}|\xi|^{2}+C_{0}\varepsilon(1+|U|_{L^{\infty}})|\xi|-\rho|\xi|^{2}}\cdot\frac{\tau_{0}^{|\varrho|}}{\varrho!}e^{\rho|\xi|^{2}}|\partial^{\varrho}A_{ij}|.

The proof is complete, under the assumption
|T_{M}^{-1}-1|\ll\rho
.
∎
